vaḻuviṉ ṟeytiyum amaiyāy  (puṟam. 99)
eṉṟum, avaṉ makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉiyai,
antarat tarumpeṟa lamiḻtam aṉṉa
karumpivaṭ ṭantōṉ perumpiṟaṅ kaṭaiyē  ( 392)
eṉṟum, pāṭiṉār auvaiyār.
iṅṅaṉam, nilavaṇikarum nīrvaṇikarum mūvēntarum, ñālattiṉ palaviṭaṅkaṭkum ceṉṟu, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa arum poruḷkaḷai yellām koṇarntu, tamiḻakattaiyum tamiḻar vāḻkkaiyai yum vaḷampaṭuttiṉar.
cīṉattiṉiṉṟu kaṟpūram kaṟkaṇṭu cīṉakkāram mutaliyaṉavum, cāliyiṉiṉṟu karumpu karāmpū tippili (paṇṭaki), mutaliyaṉavum, molukkāviṉiṉṟu aṭṭikamum (cātikkāyum), intōṉē ciyāveṉṉum kīḻintiyat tīvukkaṇattiṉiṉṟu koṭiyīntu eṉṉum cavvariciyum, malaiyāviṉiṉṟu pākku eṉṉum aṭaik kāyum, ilaṅkaiyiṉiṉṟu karuvāppaṭṭaiyum, āpu kāṉit tāṉam eṉṉum kāntārattiṉiṉṟu peruṅkāyamum, arapiyāvi ṉiṉṟu aṭappam (vātumai), koṭi muntiri, curālai (cāmpi rāṇi) mutaliyaṉavum, mēlaiyāciyāviṉiṉṟu kacakacā attiri (kōvēṟu kaḻutai) mutaliyaṉavum, ciṉṉa āciyāviṉiṉṟu kottumalli, cīrakam, peruñcīrakam, koṅkārap pū (kuṅkumappū) mutaliyaṉavum, mekacikkōviṉiṉṟu miḷakāyum, amerikkā viṉiṉṟu vaḷḷiyeṉṟum carukkaraivaḷḷiyeṉ ṟum collap paṭum cīṉikkiḻaṅkum, piṟaviṭaṅkaḷi ṉiṉṟum piṟavum, vantu cērntaṉa.
ki.mu. 1500 pōl āriyar intiyāviṟku varumvarai, tamiḻ moḻiyum tamiḻilakkiyamum tamiḻa nākarikamum tamiḻar vāḻkkai yum, immiyum āriyaṅ kalavātu muḻuttūya nilaiyil iruntu vantaṉa.
cēlai
cēlai - cēla (c)
cīrai - cīlai - cēlai (piṅ.).
vaṭavar cil (c) eṉṟoru ceyaṟkai mūlattai amaittuk koṇṭu, āṭaiyaṇital eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar.  va.va.166.
caivampaṟṟiya cila tamiḻk kuṟiyīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ
āṉaintu (pañca kavyam)
āṉaintai (tuṭicaikiḻār a. citamparaṉār kūṟukiṟapaṭi) pāl tayir veṇṇey mōr ney eṉṟu koḷḷuvatē porutta māyirukkiṟatu. āṉaintaip pañca kavyam eṉṟu moḻi peyarttuk kūṟiṉatuṭaṉ, pāl, tayir, ney, kōmūttiram cāṇam eṉṟu piṟaḻak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar āriyar.
tirunīṟu
civaperumāṉ tammai yaṭaintavariṉ tīviṉaiyai erittu viṭukiṟār eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyatē, tirunīṟṟup pūccākat terikiṉṟatu. nīṟu = cuṇṇam, poṭi. pūtiyeṉpatu nīṟṟiṉ maṟupeyar. pūḻti (puḻuti) - pūti. o.nō: pōḻtu (poḻutu) - pōtu. pūti = poṭi, tūḷ. pūtiyai vi eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuc cērttu, vipūtiyeṉṟu vaṭacollākkiṉar.
uruttirākkam
uruttira + akkam = uruttirākkam.
uruttiraṉ eṉṟu civaperumāṉuk koru peyar tamiḻilēyē yiruntatu. uruttal = ciṉattal, tōṉṟutal, urum = neruppu, ciṉam, iṭi. urumi = puḻuṅku. urumam = ucci vēḷai. uruppa = aḻala (puṟam. 35). uru eṉṟa col mutalāvatu. neruppaiyum piṉpu ciṉattaiyuṅ kuṟittatu. o.nō: aḻal - aḻalutal = ciṉattal, kaṉal - kaṉalutal = ciṉattal. ciṉam neruppiṉ taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. ciṉam ... cuṭum (kuṟaḷ. 306). neruppiṉ taṉmai oḷiyātalālum, oḷiyāl poruḷkaḷiṉ vaṭivan tōṉṟu talālum, uru eṉṉum col vaṭivaṅ kuṟittatu. cuvaiyoḷi eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, kāṭciyai allatu vaṭivattai oḷiyeṉṟatu kāṇka.
uru + u = uruvu. uruvu + am = uruvam.
uruvu - urupu (vēṟṟumai vaṭivam).
uru, uruppaṭi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, ettuṇaiyō poruḷkaḷil ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
uruvam eṉṉun tamiḻccollē, vaṭamoḻiyil rūpa eṉṟu tiriyum.
civaṉ neruppiṉ taṉmaiyum aḻipput toḻilum uṭaiya varātaliṉ, uruttiraṉ eṉappaṭṭār. uru + tiram = uruttiram (rutram-va.) = ciṉam. tiram oru toḻiṟpeyar īṟu. uruttirattai yuṭaiyavaṉ uruttiraṉ. uruttiraṉ eṉṉum tamiḻp peyaraiyē, rutra eṉṉum āriya vaṭivil ciṉakkuṟipput tōṉṟuṅ kaṭuṅkāṟṟiṟkup peyarāyiṭṭaṉar āriyar eṉka.
akkam = kūlam (tāṉiyam), maṇi, akkam - akku.
aḵkañ karukkēl eṉṟār auvaiyār. kūlam maṇiyeṉṟum poruḷpaṭuvatai, neṉmaṇi yeṉṉum vaḻakkālaṟika. 
kokkiṟa kakkam  (tiruppu. 416)
uruttiraṉpaṟṟi yaṇiyappaṭum akkam uruttirākkam. nīṭṭaṟ puṇarcci (tīrkka canti) yum, marūu muṟaiyil tamiḻukkuc ciṟupāṉmai yuṇṭeṉpatai, marāṭi, kuḷāmpal, eṉātu, kuṇātu eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷāl uṇarka.
uruttirākkattiṉ ciṟappup paṇpai, pūtanūlum (Physics) nilaittiṇai nūlum (Botany) aṟintavar ārāyntu kūṟa vēṇṭum.
uruttirākkattai ruttirākṣam eṉṟu tirittu, ruttiraṉiṉ kaṇṇiliruntu tōṉṟiyatāka oru katai kaṭṭiṉar āriyap paḻaimaiyar. iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇam. rā (Ra) eṉṉum (cūriyat) teyvattiṉ kaṇṇīrē maḻaiyeṉṟum, atiliruntu payirpaccaikaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, paṇṭai yekipatil vaḻaṅkiya oru paḻaimaik koḷkaiyai, mēlaiyāciyāvi lirunta pōtu āriyar kēṭṭaṟintatē yeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
piṟkālattil, ruttirākṣam vaṭanūṟ poruḷaiyoṭṭik kaṇmaṇiyeṉa moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭatu.
ō(m), kuru, tīkkai (tīkṣā) eṉpaṉavum tamiḻccoṟkaḷākavē tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ōṅkārattiṟku iṉṟum tamiḻ varivaṭivamē eḻutappaṭuvataiyum, uru eṉṉum col pōṉṟē kuru eṉṉum collum, veppam tōṟṟam civappu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvataiyum, tīkkai eṉpatu kuru paruvāṉmāviṉ malattaik kāṇṭal tīṇṭal mutaliyavaṟṟāl tīttu (erittu) viṭutalaik kuṟittalaiyum nōkkuka.
ō - ōṅkāram o.nō: rī - rīṅkāram.
ō = a + u eṉṟu vaṭamoḻip puṇarccippaṭi pirittatu piṟkālam. āciriyaṉ eṉpataṉ poruḷait taḻuvi. kuru eṉpatil, ku = kuṟcitam, ru = ruttiraṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu. News eṉpatu North East West South eṉṉum nāṟṟicaic ceyti kuṟippatu eṉṟu ap peyarkkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
ōm eṉṉuñ col ellāvaṟṟaiyum paṭaikkum mūla āṟṟalaik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, pātukāppup poruḷateṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, tamiḻccollē yeṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum taṭaiyillai.
ō eṉpatu uyarac cuṭṭu. atu uyaramāy vaḷartalaiyum vaḷarttalaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vaḷarttal kāttal. o.nō: ē - e - eṭu. eṭuttal = vaḷarttal. Rear, v.t. (orig.) to raise, to bring up to maturity. [A.S. roeran, to raise].
ō - ōm - ōmpu. o.nō; ē - ēm - ēmpu. ā - a - am - ampu - ampar. kum - kumpu. tirum - tirumpu.
ōm = kāppu. ōm + paṭu = ōmpaṭu. ōmpaṭu + ai = ōmpaṭai = pātukāppu, pātukāppuc ceytal. ōmpaṭutturaittaleṉpatu oru kōvaittuṟaiyāyu muḷḷatu. ōmpaṭutal taṉviṉai. ōmpaṭuttal piṟaviṉai. vaḻippaṭutturaittal eṉṉum tuṟaip peyarai nōkkuka.
	matampaṟṟiya cila potuccoṟkaḷ
māyai
māy + ai = māyai. o.nō: cāy + ai = cāyai = niḻal. māy + am = māyam. māyai māyam eṉpavai, aḻivu. mayakkam eṉṉum poruḷaṉa. māyamāyk kāṇōm, māyavittai eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka. māyai cāyai eṉṉum tamiḻc coṟkaḷai māyā cāyā eṉṟu ākāra vīṟākkiṉa vaḷavāṉē vaṭacollākak kūṟuvatu nakaippiṟ kiṭamāṉatē.
matantaḻuviya cila karuttukaḷ
vīṭu
āṉmāvukkut tuṉpattai nīkkuvatum nilaiyāṉa iruppumākiya tuṟakkavulakaippōla, veyiṉmaḻait tuṉpattai vilakkuvatum nilaiyākat taṅkaṟkuriyatumāṉatu eṉṉuṅ karuttil, illattai vīṭeṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr.
viṭu - vī = (piṟavi narakat tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum) viṭutalai, tuṟakkam, ulakappaṟṟait tuṟantu peṟuvatu tuṟakkam.



ēḻulakam
paṇṭai ñālam ēḻu tīvukaḷāy allatu kaṇṭaṅkaḷāyirunta maiyiṉ, ñālattaic cērttu mēl ēḻulakam amaintiruppa tāka muṉṉōr koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. iṉi, eḻukōḷkaḷiṉiṉṟum ēḻulaka vuṇarcci yuṇṭāṉa tākavuṅ koḷḷalām.
ēḻulakattaik kuṟippataṟkē eḻunilai māṭamum eḻunilaik kōpuramum eṭuttaṉar eṉka.
eḻu tīvukaḷaiyuñ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kaṭalkaḷai eḻukaṭal eṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr. o.nō: Indian Ocean, Atlantic Ocean (from Mount Atlas, in the northwest of Africa). eḻukaṭalkaḷai naṉṉīr, uvarnīr, pāl, tayir, ney, tēṉ, karuppañcāṟu eṉpavaṟṟāl niṟaintatākap piṟaḻak kūṟiṉar āriyap paḻaimaiyar.
eḻupiṟappu
ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyi rīṟāka āṟum, tēvar piṟap poṉṟu māka, uyiraṭaivatu ēḻ piṟappeṉṟu tamiḻar koṇṭaṉar. ivaṟṟait tēvar māntar vilaṅku paṟavai nīrvāḻvaṉa ūrvaṉa nilaittiṇai eṉa vēṟoru vakaiyāyeṇṇiyatuṭaṉ, māntarkkuḷḷēyē cūttiraṉ vaiciyaṉ kṣattiriyaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ eṉa muṟaiyē uyarnta nālvakaip piṟappuḷḷatākavum. pirāmaṇap piṟappu oru virivaḷarcci (Evolution) yeṉavum kūṟiṉar āriyap pārppaṉar.
piṟappā luyarvutāḻvu vakuppatu (intiya) āriyak koḷkai: tamiḻar koḷkaiyaṉṟu. piramāvē makkaḷai nāṟkulamākap paṭaittāreṉṟāl, ēṉai nāṭukaḷil ēṉ aṅṅaṉam paṭaikka villai? intiyāvil maṭṭum ēṉ paṭaikka vēṇṭum? atilum tamiḻarai ēṉ paṭaikkavillai? intukkaḷ kiṟitta varum ilāmiyarumāṉavuṭaṉ ēṉ piramāviṉ paṭaippu avarkaḷait tākkuvatillai? itaṉāl, piṟappāṟ ciṟappuk koḷkai āriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭa tannalak karuttēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟeṉka. intiyāviṉ oṟṟumaiyaik keṭuppatu itaippōṉṟa vēṟoṉṟumillai. ayalnāṭu ceṉṟāl kaṇtiṟakkumeṉṟa karuttuppaṟṟiyē, iṭaikkālattil intiyar ayalnāṭu celvatu āriyattāl taṭukkappaṭṭirunta teṉka.
piṟappokku mellā vuyirkkuñ ciṟappovvā
ceytoḻil vēṟṟumai yāṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 972)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
makkaḷ varavarap peruki varutalaiyum, ataṉāṟ kulamum peruki varutalaiyum, orukālattil kulamēyillā tiruntataiyum nōkkuka. kapilarakavalaiyuṅ kāṇka.
muppaṭivam (tirimūrttam)
māntarkku mutalāvatu iṟappaccampaṟṟi aḻivuṇarcciyum piṉpu kāppuṇarcciyum. ataṉpiṉ paṭaippuṇarcciyum tōṉṟuvatē iyalpu. mutalāvatu civaṉukku aḻipput toḻilum piṉpu tirumālukkuk kāpputtoḻilum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu paṭaip puṇarcci tōṉṟiyapōtu, atuvum civaṉukkē yurittākkap paṭṭatu. oṭuṅkiṉa iṭattiliruntē oṉṟu tōṉṟavēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟi.
itē,
tōṟṟiya titiyē yoṭuṅkimalat tuḷatā
manta māti yeṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṟu civañāṉa pōtattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ, paṭaipput toḻilaip pirittut taṅkaḷ kulamutalvarāya piramāviṉatu eṉṟu kūṟip paḻaimai varaintaṉar. āyiṉum tamiḻar oppuk koḷḷā maiyāl avarukkuk kōyilvaḻipā ṭēṟpaṭavillai. itaṟkup piṟkālattil oru katai kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.
pirāmaṇariṉ kulamutalvarē piramāveṉpatu, piramasrī, piramatāyam eṉṉum pirāmaṇaraik kuṟitta colvaḻakku ḷālum, vīramakēntirattilirunta piramā kantaṉoṭu pōyc cērntu koṇṭamaiyāl, aṭutta aṇṭattilirunta piramāvaic cūrapaṉmaṉ varavaḻaittāṉ eṉṉuṅ kantapurāṇac ceyti yālum uṇarappaṭum.
tirumāl mukilvaṭiviṉa rātaliṉ, mēkattaic camuttira meṉṉum taṅkaḷ maṟaivaḻakkuppaṭi, kaṭalai avarkku iṭamākkiṉar āriyar, tirumāl vaṇakkam varavarac ciṟantu vantamaiyāl, piramā tirumāliṉ makaṉeṉappaṭṭār pōlum. āriya maṟaiyōtuvatē ciṟanta kalvi eṉṉuṅ karuttil, kalaimakaḷ (caravati) piramāviṉ maṉaiviyākak kūṟappaṭṭāḷ. tamiḻar kalviyait tamiḻ mātu eṉa moḻippeyarāṟ kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟik kalaippeyarāṟ kūṟa villai. āriyam varumuṉ tamiḻoṉṟē tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkiṉa maiyiṉ.
paṇṭaikkālattil ovvōr aracaṉiṭattum, ovvoru karumāṉ allatu taccaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉē ūrkaḷai allatu nakarkaḷaik kaṭṭupavaṉātaliṉ, ulakak karumāṉ (vicuvakarmā) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ulaka viṭaikaḻi eṉpatil ulakam nakaraik kuṟittalaiyum, karumāṉ eṉpatu kollaṉ peyarātalaiyuṅ kāṇka. vicuvakarmā eṉpatu ulakak karumāṉ eṉpatiṉ moḻipeyarppē.
ulakak karumāṉukku aracat taccaṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. o.nō:  Gk. architekton - archi, chief, and tekton, a builder, E. architeet.
ūraik kaṭṭuvatu taccaṉātalālum, kollarukkuḷ karumāṉ, taccaṉ, kaṟṟaccaṉ, kaṉṉāṉ, poṟkollaṉ eṉa aim piriviruppatālum, ulakattaip paṭaittavaṉ mutaṟ karumāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ aimmukaṉ eṉṟum oru paḻaimai vaḻakku toṉṟu toṭṭuk kammāḷarkkuḷ vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇar tam kulamutalvarākiya piramaṉaip paṭaipput teyvameṉṟum nāṉmukaṉeṉṟum kūṟiyapōtu, kammāḷarkku avar mītu pakaiyuṇṭāṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. atuvē iṉṟum toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu. āriya maṟaiyai nāṉkākap pakuttatu piṟkālamātaliṉ, piramāvai nāṉmukaṉ eṉṟatu ticaipaṟṟi allatu kulappirivupaṟṟiyē yiruttal vēṇṭum.
aṟumuṟai vāḻttu
pāṭāṇporuḷ pukaḻcci, vāḻttu, vaḻuttu eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. pukaḻcciyāvatu oruvaraic ciṟappittuk kūṟal; vāḻttāvatu oruvarai nīṭu vāḻkaveṉṟal: vaḻuttāvatu oru teyvattaip parāvutal.
amararkaṇ muṭiyum aṟuvakai yāṉum (puṟat. 21) eṉpatāl, vāḻttappaṭuporuḷ āṟeṉṟār tolkāppiyar. avai muṉivarum pārppārum āṉiraiyum maḻaiyum muṭiyuṭai vēntarum ulakumām eṉṟu kūṟiṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivaṟṟuḷ, pārppār muṟkālattuk kēṟkāmaiyiṉ, avarkkup patilāyk kūṟattakkatu aṟamēyākum.
koṭinilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi yeṉṟa
vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē  (puṟat. 27)
eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpā.
itil koṭi nilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉpataṟkup palarum palavāṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉar. avaṟṟuḷ mu. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ kūṟiyatē uṇmaiyāṉa poruḷākum. atu avai muṟaiyē vāṉ nīttār aṟaṉ eṉpaṉa eṉpatu.
mutalaṉa mūṉṟum eṉṟataṉāl muṉṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa mutaṉ mūṉṟu poruḷum eṉṟum, vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ eṉṟataṉāl, avai mikunta ciṟappuṭaiyavai eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē eṉpataṉāl, avai kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu cērntuvarumeṉṟum poruḷpaṭuvatālum, mutalaṉa mūṉṟum eṉṟa cuṭṭu, amararkaṇ muṭiyum aṟuvakaiyait tavira vēṟoṉ ṟaiyum taḻuva muṭiyāmaiyāṉum, koṭi nilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa mūṉṟaṟkum aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ kūṟiya poruḷ amarar kaṇ muṭiyum aṟuvakaiyil mūṉṟāyamai talāṉum, itaṟkuc ciṟanta paḻantamiḻ nūlākiya tirukkuṟaṭ pāyiram kāṭṭā yiruttalāṉum, vēṟōruraikku iṭamillai yeṉka.
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu eṉṉum peyarum, ataṉpiṉ koṭi nilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa muṟaiyum, tirukkuṟaṭ pāyirattil amain tiruppatai nōkkuka.
com
com1 - va (i.vē.)
cummai = tokuti, kūṭṭam.
cum - com - (contu) - contam = taṉṉoṭu kūṭiyatu.
com2 - va, vām. (i.vē).
cum -cummai = tokuti, celvat tokuti.
cum - com = cottu. mutucom = muṉṉōr tēṭṭu.
com - cottu. o.nō: tokai = tokuti, celvam.
vām = cottu. tēvavām = teyvac cottu, kōyiṟ cottu.
vām - vāmi - vāmiṉ = cottukkāraṉ, āṇṭai, āṇṭavaṉ, teyvam.
vaṭavar vāmiṉ eṉṉuñ collai va + miṉ eṉṟu pakuttu, contakkāraṉ, uṭaiyavaṉ, urimaiyāḷaṉ, talaivaṉ, kaṇavaṉ, aracaṉ, kuru, teyvap paṭimai eṉṟu poruḷ toṭuppar.
colvēr kāṇvaḻikaḷ
coṟpiṟappiyal eṉpatu coṟkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟṟaik kūṟuvatē yaṉṟi olimuṟai taḻuviyum uṉṉippākavum kuṟikkōḷ koṇṭum kūṟuvataṉṟu. itai ‘Etymology’ eṉṉum peyarē uṇarttum. Gk. etumos = true; etumon = original form of a word; etumologia - E. etymology = account of facts relating to formation and meaning of word. 
coṟpiṟappiyal eṉpatu oru taṉi aṟiviyaṟ ṟuṟaiyātaliṉ. ataṟkuriya neṟimuṟaikaḷaiyellām aṟinta piṉṉarē coṟkaṭku vērkāṇum ārāycciyil īṭupaṭutal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, piṉvarumāṟu eḻuvakai vaḻumuṭipākavē muṭiyum.
1. olimuṟaic colliyal (Sound Etymology)
e-ṭu: pārāḷumaṉṟu - Parliament (F. Parleiament). pār. āḷum. maṉṟu eṉa muppeyarc col koṇṭatu tamiḻc coṟṟoṭar. Parler (to speak) eṉṉum pireñcu viṉaimutaṉilaiyum ment eṉṉum īṟum koṇṭatu āṅkilaccol. ātalāl iraṇṭum vevvēṟām.
2. uṉṉippuc colliyal (Guessing Etymology)
e-ṭu: vaṭu - vaṭai (naṭuvil tuḷaiyākiya kuṟṟamuḷḷatu). itu cariyaṉṟu. uḻuttamā picupicupput taṉmaiyuṭaimaiyāl, eḷitil eṇṇey ūṭuruvi vēkumāṟu uḻuntu vaṭaiyiṉ naṭuvil tuḷaiyiṭap peṟum. itu vaṭuvaṉṟu.
vaḷ - vaṭṭai - vaṭai - va. vaṭā. vaṭṭamāṉatu vaṭai. ituvē uṇmaiyāṉa poruḷ.
3. kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology)
e-ṭu: veṟu - veṟukkai (veṟukkappaṭattakkatu) = celvam. va. muktā (cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutalai peṟṟatu) - ta. muttam - muttu. ik kūṟṟukaḷ cariyalla. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu tuṟaviyar kūṟṟu; piṉṉatu vaṭavar kūṟṟu. veṟuttal = ceṟital, niṟaital, veṟukkai = tiraṇṭa celvam.
viṟappum uṟappum veṟippuñ ceṟivē  (tol. col. 830)
muttu = uruṇṭaiyāṉatu. muttu - muttam - va.muktā. ivaiyē uṇmai   yāṉa poruḷ.
4. aṭippaṭṭa colliyal  (Popular Etymology)
e-ṭu: uṇmai = uḷḷattoṭu (karuttoṭu) poruntiyatu. vāymai = vāyoṭu (colloṭu) poruntiyatu. meymmai = uṭampoṭu (ceyaloṭu) poruntiyatu. im muṟaip poruṭkūṟṟuc cariyaṉṟu. uṇmai = uḷḷatu. vāymai = tēṟṟamāy vāyppatu (niṟaivēṟuvatu). meymmai = uṭampu pōṟ kaṇkūṭāṉatu (substantiality). iṅṅaṉam koḷvatē poruttamāṉatu.
5. nakaiyāṭṭuc colliyal  (Playful Etymology)
e-ṭu: tōcai = cuṭumpōtu irumuṟai cai yeṉṟa ōcai kēṭpatu. iḵtu oru vēṭikkaik kūṟṟu. tōytal = māppuḷittal. tōy - tōyai - tōcai eṉpatē poruttam. tōcaiyait tōyappam eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
6. poruntap pukalvuc colliyal (Plausible Etymology)
e-ṭu: peruccāḷi = periya paṇappai pōṉṟatu. itu kāḷamēkar kūṟṟu. perutta eli = peruccāḷi eṉpatē uṇmaiyōṭu poruntiyatu.
7. aṟivāravārac colliyal (Pedantic Etymology)
kīḻ = ñālam uruḷumpōtu kiḻakkut ticaiyiṟ kīḻnōkkic celvatu; mēl = atu aṅṅaṉam uruḷumpōtu mēṟkut ticaiyil mēṉōkki eḻuvatu. itu kaṇiyar tiṟampaṭak kūṟuvatu. kīḻ = tamiḻ nilapparappuk kīḻkkōṭiyil kaṭalāṟ paḷḷamā yiruppatu. mēl = atu mēṟkōṭiyil malait toṭarāl mēṭāyiruppatu. itu kālṭuvelār kūṟṟu. ituvē uṇmai yāṉatu.
iṉi, uṇmaic colliyal neṟimuṟaikaḷum colvēr kāṇum vaḻivakaikaḷum varumāṟu : 
1. ilakkaṇa aṟivuṭaimai
ovvoru pakucoṟkum, mutaṉilai, īṟu, iṭainilai, cāriyai, puṇarcci, tiripu ākiya aṟuvakai yuṟuppukaḷ amaintuḷḷa varai pakuttaṟiyat terital vēṇṭum.
2. moḻinūlaṟivum moḻiyārāycciyum
varalāṟṟu moḻinūl (Philology) aṟivum conta moḻi yārāycciyum aṭuttu vēṇṭappeṟum iṉṟiyamaiyāt takutiyākum.
3. colliya laṟivuppēṟu
kīṟṟu (Skeat) eḻutiyuḷḷa āṅkilac colliyal neṟimuṟai kaḷaiyum (Principles of English Etymology - 2 Vols) cēmpararum (Chambers) tokutta āṅkilac colliyal (Etymological) akara mutalikaḷaiyum, kaṟṟut teḷital vēṇṭum.
4. ayaṉmoḻi yaṟivu
malaiyāḷam teluṅku pōṉṟa akappuṟa moḻikaḷaiyum, marāṭṭi inti pōṉṟa puṟamoḻikaḷaiyum, āṅkilam ilattīṉam kirēkkam pōṉṟa puṟappuṟamoḻikaḷaiyum ōraḷavu kaṟṟalum vēṇṭum.
taṉcoṟpōl tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkum vēṟṟuc collaiyum, vēṟṟuc coṟpōl piṟamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum taṉ collaiyum pirittaṟitaṟku ayaṉmoḻiyaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyāta tām.
5. perumpāl tamiḻccoṟ poruḷaṟivu
iruvakai vaḻakkilumuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum perumpālāṉa vaṟṟiṉ poruḷai aṟintiruttal vēṇṭum.
6. tamiḻ oliyiyalpaṟital
tamiḻ mutaṉmutal kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya melloli moḻiyātaliṉ, valloli mikka tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum āriya moḻikaḷaiyum aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittārāyiṉ, uṇmai kāṇa muṭiyātu. tamiḻiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa oliyiyalpaiyum coṟ ṟūymaiyaiyum aṟiya virumpuvār. iṟṟait teṉpāṇṭi nāṭākiya nellai vaṭṭāra nāṭṭuppuṟa ulaka vāḻakkai aṟital iṉṟiyamaiyātatām. tamiḻiṉ mellolikaḷē tirāviṭat tilum āriyattilum vallolikaḷāyt tirintuḷḷaṉa.
v-L: brŒ - ceyu (bj.), kampu - gumpu(bj.) kal - khal (pirā). pākam bhaga (va).
7. eḻuttukaḷiṉ tiripukaḷai yaṟital
collākka muṟaiyil, ovvoreḻuttum cilvēṟeḻuttum palvēṟeḻuttumākat tirikiṉṟatu. at tiripukaḷaiyellām aṟiyākkāl pala colvērkaḷaik kāṇṭal aritām.
e-ṭu: ḷa - ḵ : eḷku - eḵku, veḷku - veḵku.
ḷa - ka : uḷi - ukir. taḷai - takai.
ḷa - ca : uḷi - uci - ūci - va. cūci.
ḷa - ṭa : naḷi - naṭi. makaḷ - makaṭūu.
ḷa - ṇa : peḷ - peṇ, vaḷ - vaḷar - vaṇar.
ḷa - ya : toḷ - toy, māḷ - māy.
ḷa - ra : nīḷ - nīr, vaḷ - vār.
ḷa - la : koḷ - kol.
ḷa - ḻa : kāḷ - kāḻ, tuḷaci - tuḻāy.
ḷa - ṟa : teḷ - teṟu - teṟṟu, veḷ - veṟu.
ḷa - ṉa : muḷai - muṉai, vaḷai - vaṉai.
8. coṟṟiripaṟital
e-ṭu: aram (civappu) - arattam (civappu) - aratti - atti = civanta paḻam. ap paḻamaram. akal - āl = akaṉṟu paṭarum maram. kōnāy - ōnāy. tamappaṉ - takappaṉ. taku + appaṉ eṉṟu pirippatu tavaṟām.
9. coṟkaḷiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivaṟital
iṭaikaḻi - ṭēḻi (koccai) - rēḻi (koccai), maṇittakkāḷi – maṇat takkāḷi (koccai). ṭēḻi, rēḻi, maṇattakkāḷi eṉṉum koccai vaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu, vēraiyum vērpporuḷai yum aṟiyamuṭiyātu.
10. palaporu ḷoruvaṭivuc coṟkaḷaip pakuttaṟital
cila coṟkaḷ vevvēṟu poruḷkoṇṭa vevvēṟu vēriṉiṉṟu piṟantu oru vaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. avaṟṟai vēṟu paṭuttiyaṟital vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu: 
maṇi = kariyatu.
mal - māl = karumai, mukil, kariya tirumāl.
māl - māri = mukil, maḻai, kariya kāḷi.
māl - mā = karumai, mā - māyōṉ = kariya tirumāl.
māyōḷ = kariya kāḷi.
mal - maḷ - maṟu = kaḷaṅkam, karumpuḷḷi.
maḷ - (may) - mai = karumai, mukil, kārāṭu.
maḷ - maḻai = mukil, mukil nīr.
maḷ - maṇ - maṇi = nīlakkal, karumpāci.
maṇimiṭaṟṟōṉ = kariya kaḻuttuḷḷa civaṉ. maṇivaṇṇaṉ = kariya tirumāl.
maṇi2 = vaṭṭamāṉatu.
muḷ - muru - muruku = vaḷainta kātaṇi.
muru - muri - mūri = vaḷaivu.
muri - muṟi - maṟi. muru - muṟu - muṟṟu.
muṟu - muṟai - miṟai = vaḷaivu.
muḷ - (muṇ) - (muṇam) - muṭam. (muṇam) - muṇaṅku - muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam.
maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kālai vaḷaittal.
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭai = uruṇṭaiyāṉatu.
muḷ - muṇṭu = uruṇṭa kaṭṭai
muḷ - muṭi - muṭiccu.
muḷ - (muṇ) - (maṇ) - maṇi = ōcaimikka vaṭṭamāṉa veṇ kalattaṭṭu. ataippōl olikkum nāḻimaṇi. vaṭṭamāṉa kaṭikaiyāram (kaṭikāram).
maṇi3 = ciṟiyatu.
mul - muṉ - muṉi = yāṉaikkuṭṭi.
muṉ - muṉṉi, muṉṉai = ciṟupayaṟu.
mul - muḷ - muḷai - muḷaiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ.
muḷ - muṭṭu - moṭṭu - moṭṭai.
muṭṭuk kurumpai = iḷaṅ kurumpai.
moṭṭaip paiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ.
muḷ - maḷ - maḻa - maḻavu = iḷamai.
maḷ - maṇ - maṇi = ciṟiyatu, maṇikkāṭai, maṇikkākkai, maṇikkuṭal, maṇippayaṟu, maṇippuṟā mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
maṇi - māṇi = ciṟuvaṉ, paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ, maṇamākā iḷaiñaṉ.
maṇi4 = oḷiyuḷḷatu.
maṇṇutal = kaḻuvutal, miṉukkutal.
maṇṇuṟu maṇiyiṉ  (puṟam. 147)
maṇ - maṇṇi - maṇi = oḷikkal.
11. coṟkaḷaic cevvaiyāyp pirittal
uṭakkeṭuttup pōtal = uṭakku + eṭuttup pōtal (kūṭātal, tōlum elumpumātal, mika melital). itai uṭal keṭuttup pōtal eṉṟu ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akaramutali pirittiruppatu tavaṟām.
uṭaṅku - uṭakku = uṭampukkūṭu.
12. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpaṟital
ovvoru poruḷiṉ (pārttamaṭṭiṟ kaṇṇaik kavarum) ciṟappiyalpai yum uṟṟunōkkiyē, atataṟkuriya peyarai iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar muṉṉait tamiḻar.
e-ṭu: kākā(kākkā) eṉṟu karaivatu kākam(kākkai). mūṉṟu aṇikaḷai (varikaḷai) mutukil uṭaiyatu aṇil. irumai (karumai) yāṉa māṭṭiṉam erumai. vaḻuvaḻuveṉṟirukkum maram vāḻai.
muṭivēynta pēraracaṉ vēntaṉ. vēytal = muṭiyaṇital. vēyntōṉ - vēntaṉ. koṉṟai vēyntāṉai (civaṉaik) koṉṟai vēntaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya mukkuṭiyaracarkkē muṭiyaṇiyum urimai mutaṟkālattiliruntatu.
13. poruḷ varicaiyaṟital
e-ṭu: arukutal = ciṟuttal, oṭuṅkutal, aru - arai= ciṟutta allatu oṭuṅkiya iṭai. uṭampiṉ naṭu. pāti, kōl - kāl = kampam. tūṇ, tūṇpōl uṭampait tāṅkum uṟuppu. uṭampiṉ kāṟpakuti, kāṟpakuti (nāliloṉṟu).
uṭampiṉ pātiyaḷavāyiruppatu araiyeṉṟum, nāliloru pakutiyā yiruppatu kāleṉṟum, talaikīḻāyp poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvatu uṇmaikku nērmāṟām.
14. oppumaiyamaippu (Analogy) aṟital
e-ṭu: kuḻal - kuṭal. puḻal - puṭal - puṭalai - ka. paṭala. te. poṭla. va. paṭṭōlikkā, kaṭai - kaṭalai, viṭai - viṭalai - L. vitula.
mai - mayir, tai - tayir, taittal kuttutal, muṭkuttiṉāl muḷ taittatu eṉpar. pāliṟ piraimōr ūṟṟutalaip piraikuttutal eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. pirai taittatu tayir - va. tati (dadhi), i. tahī.
15. varalāṟṟaṟivaiyum ñālanūlaṟivaiyum tuṇaikkōṭal
e-ṭu: pāṇṭavar tōṉṟu muṉṉarē pāṇṭiyar teṉṉāṭṭai āṇṭu vantaṉar. pāṇṭavaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa aruccuṉaṉ tiru nīrāṭat teṉṉāṭu vantu. cittirāṅkataṉ eṉṟu pārataṅ kūṟum oru pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷai maṇantāṉ. āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ aṭi vaikku muṉṉarē, pāṇṭiyar varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmaiyiliruntu kaṇakkil kālam teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭai āṇṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷukku āriya aṭippaṭaiyil urai varainta parimēlaḻakarum. paḻaṅkuṭikkup paṭaippuk kālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭuvarum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar kuṭikaḷ eṉṟu mūvēntar kuṭikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. iṅṅaṉa miruntum, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar pāṇṭya (Pandya) eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu tirintatākac ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali neñcārap poykkiṉṟatu. itai ūḻiyiṟutic cūṟāvaḷipōṟ cuḻaṟṟi yeṟivatu kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaka varalāṟē. pāṇṭi = kāḷai, maṟavaṉ, pāṇṭi - pāṇṭiyaṉ.
cāmai paṭaippukkālan toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭil viḷaintu varum toṇ (oṉpāṉ) kūlaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. iṉṟum atu nāṭṭuppuṟa uḻavarkku uriya oḻuṅkāṉa uṇavu vakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, ataṉ peyar cyāmā eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripāka, mēṟkuṟitta poykkaḷañciyam tuṇintu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉai veṭṭi vīḻppatu ñāla nūlaṟivē.
cāmaip payirpōl oru pullum uṇṭu. atu cāmaippul eṉappaṭum. cāmai eṉṉum teṉcollaiyē vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cyāmā eṉṟu tirittut tamiḻarai ēmāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
16. iṭukuṟi tamiḻil illaiyeṉ ṟuṇartal
tamiḻtāṉē tōṉṟiya iyaṉmoḻi yātaliṉ, atiluḷḷa ellāc coṟkaḷum karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷē. cilavaṟṟiṉ vērp poruḷ pārttamaṭṭil teriyum.
e-ṭu: cuṭalai < cuṭal < cuṭu < cuḷ.
cilavaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ āḻntu āyntālaṉṟit tōṉṟā.
e-ṭu: viṉai < viḷai. iv vuṇmaikaḷaiyē.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. 640)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol. 877)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kuṟittār.
naṉṉūluraiyāciriyar iṭukuṟiyākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷellām karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷē. palā = parutta paḻattai yuṭaiyatu. paṉai = kūriya palpōṉṟa karukkumaṭṭai yuḷḷatu. poṉ = poṟpu allatu polivuḷḷatu. maram = marattatu allatu uṇarcciyaṟṟatu pōṉṟiruppatu. mā = koṭṭaikkuḷ vaṇṭuḷḷatu.
āriya moḻikaḷ tiripuṭai moḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟ pala coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tōṉṟāvāṟu tirintiruppatuṭaṉ, pala iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷum, ciṟappākac camaṟkirutattil amain tuḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl camaṟkirutattai aṭippaṭaiyāka vait tāynta mēlai moḻinūlāciriyar, ellā moḻikaḷum iṭu kuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉpatai aṭippaṭai neṟimuṟaiyākak koṇṭa vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) eṉṉum vaḻuppaṭṭa moḻiyiyal vakaiyai ulakamuḻutum parappi varukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟṟu moḻiyārāyc ciyāl camaṟkiruta iḻivum āriya ēmāṟṟamum ulakiṟku veḷip paṭṭuviṭumē yeṉṟu añcum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, putitāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷa vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl tamakkoru kēṭakamā yiruppatu kaṇṭu, ataṉai neñcāra niṉaintu vāyāra vāḻtti vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaraṭiyārāṉa koṇṭāṉmārum tamiḻaik kāṭṭik koṭuppataṟku itu talaiciṟanta vaḻiyā yiruppataṟintu, itaṉait talaimēl tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
oru poruḷai eṅkeṅkun tēṭiyum kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟu muḻu nampikkai koṇṭavaṉukku, eṅṅaṉ tēṭaṉ muyaṟci piṟakkum? ellāc collum iṭukuṟikaḷē yeṉṟum, ellā moḻi kaḷum āyiram āṇṭiṟkorumuṟai aṭiyōṭu māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum nampukiṉṟavaṉukku eṅṅaṉ moḻiyārāycci vēṭkai eḻum? ātalāl, vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl aṟivu vaḷarccikku māperu muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyeṉṟu aṟavē puṟakkaṇikka.
17. pakuttaṟivaip payaṉpaṭuttal
e-ṭu: vaṭavai = vaṭamuṉaiyil tōṉṟum oruvakai neruppu allatu oḷi. vaṭam - vaṭavai (piṅ) = vaṭavaṉal. vaṭavaik kaṉalai vaittūti (antakak kavi vīrarākavar taṉippāṭal). 
vaṭam - vaṭantai = vaṭakāṟṟu. vaṭavait tī.
cuṭarnteri vaṭantait tīyum  (kāñcippu. irupat. 384)
akkaṭaliṉmītu vaṭavaṉal niṟka vilaiyō  (tāyumāṉavar)
o.nō: Aurora Borealis = Northern Light.
iṅṅaṉam vaṭavai eṉpatu teṉcolleṉṟum vaṭamuṉai neruppiṉ peyareṉṟum teḷivāyirukkavum, atai vaṭavā eṉṟu tirittup peṇkutirai eṉṟu poruḷuraippar vaṭavar, ittakaiya colliyalkaḷiṟ pakuttaṟivaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
18. añcāmai
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷai vaṭavar kaṭaṉkoṇṭirup patāl, avaṟṟaiyellām teṉcolleṉṟu oppukkoḷḷiṉ, camaṟkirutattiṟkut tamiḻē mūlam eṉpatu veṭṭaveḷiyām. ataṉāl, atai maṟaittaṟkup pala coṟkaṭkup poruntap pukalalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum poruḷ kūṟiyum, cilavaṟṟai iṭukuṟiyeṉṟu muttiraiyiṭṭum varukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟai maṟuttu uṇmai kūṟupavarkkup patavi allatu aluval pōyviṭum eṉṉum accam tamiḻar uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruppatāl, vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟ kaṭku vērkāṇat taṉimaṟam vēṇṭuvatu orutalai.
19. naṭunilai
kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi oruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭaiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum  (aṟaneṟi. 22)
kavi eṉpatu vaṭacol. pā, ceyyuḷ eṉpaṉavē teṉcol. ātalāṟ kaviñaṉaip pāvalaṉ eṉṟum, kaviyaraṅkaic ceyyu ḷaraṅku allatu pāvaraṅku eṉṟumē collutal vēṇṭum.
kavintukoḷvatu kavi eṉṟum, taruvatu tarumam eṉṟum, nāyai vaittiruppavaṉ nāyaṉ eṉṟum, uḷtaṇamāyiruppatu uṭṭaṇam eṉṟum, iravukacital irakaciyam eṉṟum, periya vittu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vāṉvittu eṉpatu vittuvāṉ eṉṟu talaimāṟiya teṉṟum, ivai pōṉṟu piṟavum kūṟuva tellām aṟivu muṟaippaṭṭaṉa vallaveṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
20. poṟumai
cila coṟkaṭku vērkāṭṭum uṟavuc coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭi ruppatāl, avaṟṟiṉ vēr kāṇṭaṟku nāṭpalavaṉṟi āṇṭu palavum ākalām. ātalāl, collārāyccikku mikunta poṟumaiyum vēṇṭum.
āṇṭu palavākiyum vērkāṇa viyalātu pōkavum nērum. ataṉāl uḷḷan taḷartal kūṭātu.
neṟiyaṟintu āra amara ārāyiṉ, iṟṟait tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ nūṟṟiṟ keḻupattaintiṟku vērkāṇa viyalum. eṉiṉum iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyē ovvoruvarkkum ovvoru tuṟaiyil āṟṟalamaiyum eṉpatai niṉaivil iruttutal vēṇṭum.
vāṉkuruvi yiṉkūṭu vallarakkut tolkaṟaiyāṉ
tēṉcilampi yāvarkkuñ ceyyaritāl - yāmperitum
vallōmē yeṉṟu valimaicola vēṇṭāṅkāṇ
ellārkkum ovvoṉ ṟeḷitu. 
(auvaiyār)  (centamiḻc celvi mārccu 1967)
colavam
colavam - clōka
col - colavu = marapuk kūṟṟu, paḻamoḻi, paḻamoḻi pōṉṟa ceyyuḷ toṭar.
colavu - colavam. colavu - colavaṭai.
vaṭamoḻiyil mutal vaṉappu (ātikāviyam) vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇam eṉpar. vāṉmīki muṉivar kāṭṭil oru vēṭaṉāṟ kollappaṭṭa paṟavaiyaik kaṇṭu varuntiyapōtu tam mutaṟ ceyyuḷaip pāṭiyatāl, varuttattaik kuṟikkum cōka(m) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu taṉic ceyyuṭkuc clōka eṉṟu peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu vaṭavar kūṟuvatu marapu. mā. vi. a. itai maṟuttuc cru (kēḷ) eṉṉum colloṭu toṭarpu koṇṭatāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutu kiṉṟatu.  (va.va. 167).
coli
coli - jval (vē.)
cul - culli = aṭuppu. cuḷḷeṉal = veyil cuṭutal. cuḷ - cuḷḷai = maṭkalañ cuṭumiṭam, kāḷavāy. cul - col - coli. colittal = erital, oḷirtal. col - coṉ - coṉṉam = taṅkam. col = poṉṉiṟamāṉa nel. caṭaiccennel poṉ viḷaikkum (naḷa. cuyamvara. 68). o.nō: nil - nilā, nilavu. niṟṟal = viḷaṅkutal. nil - nel = viḷaṅkum poṉpōṉṟa kūlam.  (va.va. 167:168).
coṟkulamuṅ kuṭumpamum
makkaḷaippōṉṟē coṟkaḷum kuṭumpaṅ kuṭumpamākavum kulaṅkulamāka vum toṭarpuṟṟu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru moḻiyum oru nāṭum. ovvoru vērvaḻic coṟṟokutiyum oru kulamum, ovvōr aṭivaḻic coṟṟokuti yum oru kuṭumpamum pōlvaṉa.
oru nāṭṭāṉ ayalnāṭu ceṉṟu pallāṇṭu taṅkik kuṭiyurimai peṟṟuviṭiṉum. avaṉ ayaṉmaiyai maṟaikka muṭiyātu. yārēṉum maṟaikka muyaliṉ, varalāṟṟārāycci atai veḷippaṭuttiviṭum. aṅṅaṉamē, orumoḻic collum piṟamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻakkūṉṟiṉum ataṉ ayaṉmaiyai maṟaikka muṭiyātu; yārēṉum maṟaikka muyaliṉ, varalāṟṟā rāycci ataṉai veḷippaṭuttiviṭum.
iv vīruṇmaikaḷaiyum viḷakkumāṟu oru coṟkulattaiyum mūṉṟu coṟkuṭumpaṅkaḷaiyum īṇṭuk kāṭṭuvōm.

marakkaṇṇō maṇṇāḷvār kaṇ  (perunto. 634)

uppuk kaṇṭam, kaṇṭaṅ kaṇṭamāy naṟukkutal mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.

kaḷḷoṟṟik kaṇcāy pavar  (kuṟaḷ. 927)
kāṇ - A.S. cunnan, to know; E. cunning, knowing; M.E. con, to study carefully, Goth. kunnan, to know, O.H.G. chann.
kāṇ eṉṉum teṉcol kṇā - kṉā eṉṟu cila āriya moḻikaḷil māṟiyum. ‘gna’ eṉṟu cila āriya moḻikaḷil māṟit tirintum. jñā eṉṟu vēta āriyattil mēlum tirintum vaḻaṅkum.
A.S. cnawan; Ice. kna; E. know; L. gna, Gk. gno; Skt. jna; Russ. jna.
piṟkālattu āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ‘gna’ aṭiyum (e-ṭu: cognisance, prognosticate, ignorance) muṟkālattu āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ‘con’ allatu ‘kon’ aṭiyum iruppataiyum, jñāṉam eṉṉum camaṟkirutac col uṭpaṭa ‘know’ eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkiṉa māṉa ellāvaṟṟukkum ‘kon’ eṉpataiyē mūlamāka mēlai moḻinūlār kuṟittiruppataiyum, kāṇ eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ iyalpaiyum, ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa āriyac coṟkaḷiṉ tiripaiyum, avaṟṟuḷ camaṟkirutac collum āriyac collum aṭaintuḷḷa tiripiṉ kōṭiyaiyum uṟṟu nōkkuka.
tamiḻk kaṇṇiliruntu camaṟkirutam ñāṉam peṟṟatu eṉpatu, collaḷa vilum poruḷaḷavilum uṇmaiyāyiruttal nōkkattakkatu.

vaku eṉṉum aṭiyiṉkīḻk kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷellām. koṭpāṭṭaṉ - pāṭṭaṉ - makaṉ - pēraṉ - koṭpēraṉ eṉṟa toṭarpu pōlat toṭarpupaṭṭi ruppataiyum. vaku eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa paku eṉṉum aṭiyē vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅku vataiyum atuvum bhaj eṉṟa tirivaṭiviṟ kāṭṭappaṭuvataiyum. paku eṉpataṉ aṭippiṟanta vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ teṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ pōlap palkiyum toṭarpupaṭṭum irāmai yaiyum. paku eṉṉum orē yaṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa pakkam, pāku, pākam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil muṟaiyē paksha, bahu, bhaga eṉa vevvēṟu mutaleḻuttuṭaṉ vaḻaṅku vataiyum, cevvaṉ āyntu kāṇka.

pēṭi.
itukāṟum, mūṉṟu coṟkuṭumpaṅkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
kal eṉṉum vēraṭic coṟṟokutikaḷ kāḻ kaṇ eṉṉum aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantu karumai eṉṉum vērkkaruttu maṟaintu, viḷaiyāṭu, eri, pār, kā mutaliya viṉaip poruḷ kaḷaip peṟṟa, kaḷi kāy kaṇ kāṇi mutaliya coṟkaḷaik koṇṭa tokutiyātaliṉ, coṟkulameṉṟum, īṇṭuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa  til vaku vīḻ eṉṉum mūvaṭic coṟṟokutikaḷ, tiṇmai, pakuppu, viruppam ākiya mūlak karuttukaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟaiyē taḻuviya coṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷātaliṉ, coṟ kuṭumpameṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
oru kuṭumpam pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvittalum. ap pala kuṭumpamuṅ cērntu oru kulamātalum pōla, oru coṟkuṭum pamum pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷait tōṟṟavittalum, ap pala kuṭumpamuñ cērntu oru kulamātalum uṇṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tiṉ, tiri, tiruku, tirumpu, tiṟampu, timiṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷōṭu cērkkap paṭiṉ, til kuṭumpamum oru kulamāki viṭum.
kuṭumpu, kuṭumpam, kuṭi, kulam, pāl, iṉam, varaṇam eṉa mēṉmēl viriyum makkaṭ kūṭṭap pakuppu, coṟkaḷukkum ēṟkum, ip pakuppukaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum ciṟiyatu periyatu eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
coṟperuṅ kulam
makkaṭṭokuti, kuṭumpam mutal varaṇamvarai palvakait tokutiyākap pakukkappeṟum.
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum maṇamākāta makkaḷumākak kūṭivāḻuṅ kūṭṭam kuṭumpākum. peṟṟōrum maṇamākip piḷḷaipeṟṟa makkaḷumākak kūṭivāḻuṅ kūṭṭam kuṭumpamākum. ammīṟu perumaip poruḷ vikutiyātaliṉ, kuṭumpiṟ periyatu kuṭumpameṉṟum, ivviru peyarkaḷum ulakavaḻakkil taṭumāṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum. ammīṟu perumaipporuḷ vikutiyātalai, kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam. kampu - kampam eṉṉum peyariṇaikaḷiṟ kāṇka.
pala talaimuṟaiyākat toṭarntuvarum kuṭumpam kuṭiyākum. kuṭiyaik kōttira meṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. toṭarpuḷḷa pala kuṭikaḷ cērntatu kulamākum. ceṭṭiyār mutaliyār eṉpaṉa kulaṅkaḷ. kuṟavar. maṟavar, iṭaiyar, kuṭiyāṉavar (uḻavar), cempaṭavar eṉpaṉa mutaṟkālattil aintiṇaik kuṭikaḷāka viruntu, piṉpu aiṅkulaṅkaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa.
taṉittavarum tokutiyāṉavarumākat toḻilotta pala kulaṅkaḷ allatu kulattār cērntatu pālākum. antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa tamiḻakattu nāṟpālkaḷ. antaṇar eṉpār, aruḷ mutirnta muṉivar, kuṭiyāṉavar, kavuṇṭar, akampaṭiyar mutaliya uḻutuṇṇuṅ kulaṅkaḷum mutaliyār. veḷḷāḷar mutaliya uḻuvit tuṇṇuṅ kulaṅkaḷum cērntatu vēḷāṇ pālākum, iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum. 
pala pālkaḷ cērntatu iṉam, tamiḻar, teluṅkar, kaṉṉaṭar, malaiyāḷiyar mutaliyaṉa iṉaṅkaḷ, iṉattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭatu varaṇam, āriyar, tirāviṭar, maṅkōliyar mutaliyar varaṇaṅkaḷ, varaṇam eṉṉum colliṉ iyaṟporuḷ niṟam eṉpatē. ātalāl, niṟa vēṟupāṭṭai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatē varaṇap pakuppākum. āriyar veṇṇiṟattar; maṅkōliyar poṉṉiṟattar; tirāviṭar pukar niṟattar.
makkaṭ kalappālum taṭpaveppanilai vēṟupāṭṭālum varaṇattāriṉ niṟam māṟuvatuṇṭu.
kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam varai ovvoru tokutiyum aḷavum toṭarpum paṟṟic ciṟiyatum periyatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
makkaḷpōṉṟē coṟkaḷum kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam varai palavakait tokutikaḷākap pakuttaṟ kēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām, tamiḻaraippōl kuṭumpu mutal pāl varai aivakait tokutikaḷākap pakukkap peṟum.
tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻika ḷellām iṉamoḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiluḷḷa potuccoṟ kaḷellām iṉac coṟkaḷākum.
tirāviṭac collōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa āriyac coṟkaḷum, cittiyac coṟkaḷum. avai pōṉṟa piṟavum, varaṇam eṉṉum tokutippaṭṭaṉa. ātitirāviṭa makkaḷ taṭpaveppanilai vēṟupāṭṭāl eṅṅaṉam āriya rāṉārō, aṅṅaṉamē avar coṟkaḷum āriyamāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉka.
ik kaṭṭuraiyiṉ toṭakkattil kāṭṭappeṟṟa coṟkulattil, mutaliliruntu kaṉṟu - aṉṟu eṉpatu varaiyum oru coṟ kuṭumpamākum.
ataṉoṭu kaḷ - kaḷam eṉpatu varaiyuḷḷa pakutiyaic cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkuṭumpamākum.
ataṉoṭu kaṟu - kāṟu eṉpatuvarai cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkuṭi yākum; ataṉoṭu iṟutivarai cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkulamākum.
kal eṉṉum vēr, kul eṉṉum vēriṉ tiripākum. collākkat tirivil ciṟupāṉmai ukaram akaramākat tirital uṇṭu.
e-ṭu: muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku kurum - karam (inti) kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu kuṭam - kaṭam (vaṭamoḻi).
Butt (muṭṭu) cuckoo (kukkū) mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ukaram akaramāka olippatu ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē.
kul eṉṉum vērccoṟ poruḷ poruntal allatu kalattal.

pala poruḷkaḷ allatu pakutikaḷ oṉṟu cērum pōtu tiraṭciyum ataṉāl uruṭciyum uṇṭākum.
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oṭṭāp poruḷāyiṉ tiraṭcimaṭṭum uṇṭākumeṉṟum, oṭṭum poruḷāyiṉ tiraṭciyōṭu uruṭciyum uṇṭākumeṉṟum pakuttaṟital vēṇṭum. makkaṭṭiraḷaiyum maṭṭiraḷaiyum niṉaittuk kāṇka.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭai = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa erutu.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭaṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, uṭal valimaiyāl piṟarai varuttupavaṉ.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭāṉ = tiraṇṭa taṭi.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā = tiraṇṭa vaṭivāṉa kalam.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.

kal - kalvi, kalavai, kalappu.
kala - kalampu - kalampam - katampam.
kalampu - kalampakam.
kala - kalakam = palar kalantu ceyyum caṇṭai.
kaikalattal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
kala - kalām, o.nō: poru - pōr.
porutal = poruntutal, kalattal.
kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam.
kalaṅkal = pala poruḷkaḷ kūṭutal. ataṉāl uṇṭākum mayakkam. 
maṇ tūci mutaliya piṟa poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ kalanta nīr kalaṅkal nīrākum. pala poruḷkaḷ kalantirukkum pōtu. avaṟṟuḷ vēṇṭiyataip pirittaṟiya muṭiyāta kalakkam ēṟpaṭutaliṉ, kalattaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kalakkak karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
atuvō, ituvō, aṉṟi vēṟetuvō eṉṟippaṭi oṉṟait tuṇiya muṭiyātavāṟu pala karuttukaḷ maṉattiṟ kalattal maṉakkalakkam eṉṟaṟika.
kavarvaḻikaḷaik kāṇumpōtu vaḻip pōkkaṉukkum, orunilaippaṭṭavar palar kūṭiyirukkum pōtu avaruḷ oruvaraik kāṇa virumpum putiyōṉukkum maṉakkalakkam ēṟpaṭutal kāṇka.
naḷaṉ vaṭivil nālvaraik kaṇṭa tamayanti kalakkattaiyum niṉaittuk kāṇka.
kalakkam eṉpataṉ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷāṉa mayakkam, maruḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷum itē moḻipporuṭ kāraṇattaik koṇṭavaiyē.
mayaṅku - mayakkam, mayaṅkutal = kalattal, kalaṅkutal.
tiṇaikaḷum, vēṟṟumaikaḷum tammuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalattal tiṇaimayakkam eṉṟum vēṟṟumai mayakkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. pakalum iravum kalakkum mālaiyai mayaṅkupoḻutu eṉpar. itu nūlvaḻakkil maruṇmālai eṉappaṭum.
iṉi, oḷiyiṉmaiyālō, kātalarp pirintamaiyālō makkaḷ maṉaṅkalaṅkum mālai maruṇmālai eṉiṉumām.
maruṭci = mayakkam. maru - maruḷ. maruvutal = poruntutal, kalattal.
maru - marumam - marumar - mammar = mayakkam.
Confusion eṉṉum āṅkilac collum kalattal eṉṉuṅ karuttaṭiyākavē mayakkap poruḷaik koṇṭatākum. (con = together, fuse = mix).
kala - kaluḻ - kaluri. kaluḻtal = kalattal, kalaṅkutal. kaluḻi = kalaṅkal nīr, maiyoṭu kalanta allatu maṉaṅkalaṅki yaḻum kaṇṇīr.
kalakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu iruṭkaruttum. iruṭkaruttiṉiṉṟu karumaik karuttum tōṉṟum. aṟivu akavoḷi yātaliṉ, aṟiyāmai allatu mayakkam akaviruḷ eṉappaṭum. oṉṟaṉ uṇmai yaṟiyātu kalaṅkum kalakkam poruḷ teriyāta iruḷpōṉṟa nilaiyē. ‘To be left in the dark’ eṉpar āṅkilarum, iruḷ karuniṟam ākaiyāl, kalakkap poruṭcol karumaip poruḷait taḻuviṟṟu.
kal - kāl = karuppu. kal - kaṉṟu = veyilāṟ karuku.
oru coṟku oru poruṇmai. uttikkup poruntuvatōṭu oppumaiyāl uṟutippaṭa vēṇṭum. kalattaṟporuḷ tarum kal eṉṉum col karumaipporuḷ kuṟittal pōṉṟē. ataṉ oruporuṭ collāṉa maya eṉṉum collum karumaip poruḷ kuṟikkum. mayaṅku eṉṉum colliṉ vēr maya eṉpatē. kalaṅku eṉṉum colliṉ vēr kala eṉṟiruttal kāṇka.
maya - mayaṅku - macaṅku.
maya = mayal - māl. mayal - maiyal = kātal mayakkam.
māl = mayakkam, karumai, kariya mēkam, kariya viṇṭu (viṣṇu).
māl - maḻai, māl - māri = kariyāḷ, kāḷi, maḻai.
māl - mālam = mayakkam, ēmāṟṟam.
māl - mālai = mayakkam, palaporuḷ kalanta varicai, mayaṅkum vēḷai. mālai - mālā (va.).
māl - mā - mai. māmai = karumai. māyōṉ = kariyaṉ. viṇṭu. 
o.nō: E. malbino = kariyaṉ.
mai = karuppu, mēkam. veḷḷāṭu (kārāṭu), kariya pacai allatu kuḻampu, pacaipperuḷ.
mai - maci - macaku = vaṇṭi mai.
kāṭcipporuṭ karuttu, karuttupporuṭ karuttu eṉṉum iruvakaik karuttuḷ, muṉṉatiṉiṉṟu piṉṉatu tōṉṟuvatē iyalpāyiṉum, iṉṉatiṉiṉṟē iṉṉatu tōṉṟavēṇṭu meṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. ivviraṇṭuḷ etaṉiṉṟum etuvum tōṉṟalām. allākkāl collākkam peritum muṭṭuppaṭum.
ik kaṭṭuraiyiṉ mutaṟ pakutiyil varaiyappeṟṟa kal eṉṉum coṟkulattōṭu, ataṉ muṟpaṭaiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa pakutiyaic cērttukkoḷḷiṉ, orucoṟ peruṅkula mākum. ellāt tamiḻc coṟkaḷum perumpālum ā ī ū ē ō eṉṉum ainneṭilkaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟantiruppatāl avvaintum coṟpālaṭikaḷākum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, makkaḷ pōṉṟē coṟkaḷum, kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam īṟākap palvakait tokutikaḷāka virintu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟam, makkaṭkuḷḷa toṭarpu pōṉṟē avar coṟkaṭkum moḻi kaṭkum toṭarpuṇṭeṉṟum naṭunilaiyākavum neṟippaṭavum nuṭpamākavum āyntu nōkku vārkkuc coṟṟoṭarpum moḻittoṭarpum pulaṉāvatu tiṇṇameṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
aṭiccol kāṇvaḻi
pala maraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṉṉip piṇaintu koṇṭi ruppiṉ, pārttamaṭṭil avaṟṟiṉ aṭikaḷaik kāṇpatu aritākum. avaṟṟiṉ nuṉikkoḻuntiliruntu iṇukkum. vaḷārum, kuccum, pōttum, ciṉaiyum, kompum, kiḷaiyum kavaiyumāka ovvoṉṟāyk kīḻnōkkip pirittukkoṇṭē variṉ, iṟutiyil, nilattiṉmēl mutaṟ kīḻppakutiyākavuḷḷa aṭiyaik kāṇalām. ataṉpiṉ avaṟṟiṉ vēraik kāṇpatu tēṟṟamāyiṉam, nilattait tōṇṭiṉā laṉṟik kāṇa muṭiyātu, ip paṉmarap piṇaiyal pōṉṟē. pala aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu kavaittuk kiḷaittup palkip perukiyuḷḷa coṟṟokuti kaḷum, pārtta maṭṭil pirittuṇarappaṭāvāṟu, tammuḷ mayaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyaik kāṇpataṟkum mēliruntu kīḻnōkki varal vēṇṭum. aṭiyaik kaṇṭapiṉ, āḻnta ārāycciyiṉālaṉṟi vēraik kāṇa muṭiyātu. itaik kuṟittaṟkē.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. peyar. 1)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyar.
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol. uri. 96)
eṉṟu varaiyiṭṭār.
collaṭi kāṇvaḻiyaik kāṭṭumukamāka, kōṭu eṉṉum collai eṭuttukkoṇṭu. ataṉ aṭiyai īṇṭu āykiṉṟēṉ.
kōṭu eṉṉum col, kōṇutal allatu vaḷaital eṉṉum poruḷaik koṇṭu, peyarākavum viṉaiyākavum vaḻaṅkum col. itaṉ vaḻiyākac cila coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
vaḷaitaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu vaṭṭak karuttum, vaṭṭak karuttiṉiṉṟu cūḻtaṟ karuttum tōṉṟum. orē poruḷ vaṭṭamāka, mēṉmēl vaḷaintu celvatu tirukalmuṟukalāṉa puritalātaliṉ, vaḷaivuk karuttiṉiṉṟu puritaṟ karuttum tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu:	vaḷai - vaḷaiyam (vaṭṭam)
		vaḷai - vaḷaical (cūḻvu)
		vaḷai - vaḷai (caṅkiṉ vala iṭap purivu)
kōṭutal = vaḷaital, kōṭṭam = vaḷaivu, matilāl vaḷaiyappaṭṭa kōvil allatu ciṟaiccālai, vēliyāl vaḷaiyappaṭṭa māṭṭuk koṭṭil.
kōṭṭai = vaḷainta matil, vaṭṭamāṉa neṟkūṭu, matiyaic cūḻntuḷḷa ūrkōḷ.
kōṭṭakam = vaḷainta kuḷakkarai, karai.
kōṭu = vaḷaivu, vaḷainta vari, vari.
kōṭu = vaḷainta marakkiḷai, kiḷai.
kōṭu = vaḷainta kompu, kompu.
kōṭu = vaḷainta karai, karai.
kōṭu = vaḷainta caṅku, caṅku.
malaiyaik kuṟikkum kōṭu eṉṉum col kuvaṭu eṉṉum colliṉ tiripātaliṉ vēṟām.
kōṭṭam eṉṉum teṉcollē, vaṭamoḻiyil kōṣṭa eṉṟum ilattīṉil castrum eṉṟum ataṉ vaḻiyāy āṅkilattil ‘caster’, ‘chester’ eṉṟum tiriyum. āṅkilat tirivukaḷ ūrppeyarīṟākavum vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu: doncaster, colchester, exeter (excester).
ip peyarkaḷai, aṟuppukkōṭṭai, paṭṭukkōṭṭai mutaliya tamiḻnāṭṭu ūrppeyarkaḷuṭaṉ oppunōkkuka.
kōṭu eṉpataṉ nērmūlam kōṇ.
o.nō: pāṇ - pāṭu, pēṇ - pēṭu.
kōṇ eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu, kōṇu, kōṇal, kōṇam, kōṇai mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
kōṇam eṉṉum teṉcollē, kirēkkattil ‘gonia’ (angle) eṉṟum, ataṉ vaḻiyāy āṅkilat toṭarccoṟkaḷil gon eṉṟum tiriyum.
e-ṭu: Trigonometry (tirikōṇa māttirai)
polygon (palakōṇam)
