pitirārccitam			mutucom
mēkam			mukil, kār, koṇṭal
lāpam			ūtiyam
naṭṭam			iḻappu
maittuṉaṉ			aḷiyaṉ
varucam			āṇṭu
tirī, puruṣar		āṭavar, peṇṭir
iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa vaṭa coṟkaḷ viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭup perumpālum taṟpava vaṭiviṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nakaiccuvaiyai hāya racam eṉpatum, avaiyaic cata eṉpatum. paṇiviṭaiyaic cicuruṣai eṉpatum, tirumaṇattaip pariṇayam eṉpatum, kuṭamūkku (kuṭantai), kuraṅkāṭutuṟai, ciṟṟampalam (tillai), paḻamalai (mutukuṉṟam), mayilāṭutuṟai, maṟaikkāṭu mutaliya centamiḻ nāṭṭūrt taṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷai, muṟaiyē, kumpakōṇam. kapitalam, citamparam, viruttācalam, māyūram, vētāraṇiyam eṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷāy ēṟkeṉavē, māṟṟiyiruppatum, ittakaiya iḻi ceyalai iṉṟum kaiyāḷvatum, teṉṉāṭṭu vaṭamoḻi yāḷariṉ varaiyiṟanta vaṭamoḻi veṟiyai yaṉṟi vēṟetaik kāṭṭum?
iṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟku vēṇṭiyavu malla; tamiḻar virumpiyavu malla. uṭampiṟkuṭ pukuntu ataṉukku ūṟuceyyum naccup puḻukkaḷaiyum iṉṉāp poruḷkaḷaiyum ataṉ nalattiṉ poruṭṭu nīkkuvatu pōṉṟē. vēṇṭā vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vilakkutal vēṇṭum. itaṉait taṭuppavar puṟappakaivarum uṭpakaivarumāyēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacoṟkaṭku iṅkuk kūṟiyatu piṟa coṟkaṭkum okkum.



2. putuc coṟpuṉaivu
pala poruḷkaḷ pal vakaiyil vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟ kellām potuvāka oru taṉmai kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. uyirikaḷ oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟai allatu palavaṟṟaip piṟappippatu pōṉṟē karuttukaḷum oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟai allatu palavaṟṟait tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟaṉa. iv vīruṇmaikaḷaiyum kaṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar. ōraṭiyiṉiṉṟē īṟṟu vēṟu pāṭṭāṟ palvēṟu coṟkaḷait tirittu. avaṟṟāṟ paṟpala poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
e.kā.
veḷ

veḷ - viḷ - viḷa (veḷḷait tōṭṭuk kaṉi) viḷam, viḷā, viḷavu, viḷātti viḷar. 
viḷarppu (veḷuppu)
viḷ - vil - vilvu. vilvam. villam (kūviḷam)
viḷ - viḷa - viḷaṅku, viḷakku, viḷakkam.

veḷ - veṭṭu (miṉ) veṭṭu - veṭṭi (kūliyillā veṟumai vēlai)
veṭṭam (veḷiccam)
veṭṭa (teḷivāṉa)
veṭṭai (veḷi, veṟumai, payaṉiṉmai).
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷellām, veṇṇiṟattaik kuṟikkum veṇ eṉṉum orē yaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantu. veṇmaik karuttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa palvēṟu kāṭcip poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ karuttup poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippaṉavākum. iv vaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta vēṟu cila coṟkaḷumuḷa. avai virivañci iṅku viṭappaṭṭuḷa.
cila poruḷkaṭkup piṟavaṟṟiṟ killāta ciṟappiyalpuṇṭu. ataṉaiyum nam muṉṉōr kaṇṭu. ataṟkēṟpap peyarkaḷai amaittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.kā. vāḻai (aṭi vaḻavaḻaveṉṟiruppatu).
iṅṅaṉamē piṟa moḻiyāḷarum tattam moḻiyiṟ coṟkaḷai ākkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ vērp poruḷaiyō avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpaiyō aṟintukoḷḷiṉ. aṟiviyalum kammiyamum paṟṟiya ellāc coṟkaḷaiyum eḷitāyt tamiḻil amaittuk koḷḷalām. coṟṟiripiṟ kēṟṟa īṟukaḷ tamiḻil ērāḷamāka vuḷḷaṉa. toḻiṟ peyarīṟukaḷ maṭṭum tal, al, am, ai, kai, vai, ku, pu, u, ti, ci, vi, uḷ, pāṭu, kāṭu, aravu, āṉai, mai, tu, il, āl, aṉai, aṉam, iṉam, aṇam, iṇam, aṭam, iṭam, aram, itam, mutaliyaṉavāka muppatiṟkum mēluḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟoṭu. viṉai mutalīṟukaḷ ceyappaṭu poruḷīṟukaḷ mutaliya īṟṟuvakaikaḷaiyum, kuṟumaip poruḷ. perumaip poruḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟiya muṉ piṉ ōṭṭu vakaikaḷaiyum, cērppiṉ ettuṇaiyō palavākum. ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu āṅkilam kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa īṟukaḷaiyum oṭṭukaḷaiyum otta colluṟuppu vaḷam, tamiḻ oṉṟilēyē iyalpāka amaintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
electricity eṉṉum āṅkilac col, ampar eṉṉum naṟumaṇap  poruḷaik kuṟikkum electron eṉṟa kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. ampar eṉpatu tamiḻil ōrkkōlai eṉavum peṟum. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa aṭiyārkku nallāruraiyil ampar eṉṉuñ col vantiruttalāl. ataiyum teṉ colleṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. miṉṉāṟṟalaik kuṟittaṟku ampar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu āmpariyam eṉṟoru collait tirittuḷḷaṉar putuc cērivāṇar. ceṉṉai nāṭṭut tamiḻil miṉcāram eṉṉuñ col ēṟkeṉavē puṉaiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu. atai miṉ(ṉam) eṉṟum māṟṟikkoḷḷalām.
Radio eṉṉum āṅkilac col. Radius eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintatu. ilattīṉ poruḷ.  taṇṭu, ārai, katir eṉpaṉa. katir eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu katiram allatu katiriyam eṉṟoru collai nām tirittukkoḷḷalām.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ allatu kalai muṟaikaḷiṉ peyar, avaṟṟaik kaṇṭupiṭitta āṭpeyarāl allatu iṭappeyarāl amain tuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai appaṭiyē varipeyarttu vaittuk koḷḷalām.
e.kā.
galvanism eṉṉum miṉṉākka muṟai kālvaṉi (Galvani) eṉpavarāṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.
galvanization = kālvaṉittal. galvanism kālvaṉiyam. bauxite eṉṉum maṇ vakai pirāṉcu nāṭṭil pākkacu (Baux) eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataip pākkaci eṉṟu varipeyarttuk koḷḷalām.
chemistry eṉṉum āṅkilac col. alckemy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirintatu. itu al-kimia eṉṉum arapic colliṉ tiripu. al = the (anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu); kimia eṉpatu khemia eṉpataṉ tiripu. itu ekiptu (Egypt) nāṭṭup peyariṉ kirēkka vaṭivam. ākavē chemistry eṉpataik kemiyam eṉṟu collalām.
chemist = kemiyaṉ, kemiyar
chemical = kemiya.
cila poruṭ peyarkaḷ aḷavupaṟṟi amaintuḷḷaṉa. duoderum eṉpatu paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉṉum ilattīṉ eṇṇup peyar. atu āṅkilattil paṉṉiru viral (aṅkulam) aḷavuḷḷa ciṟu kuṭaṟ pakutiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. itaip paṉṉīram eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkalām.
Furlong eṉṉum āṅkilac col paṭaiccāl nīḷam eṉṉum poruḷatu. fur = furrow (paṭaiccāl). paṭaiccāl eṉpatu aṇaippu eṉṟum collappaṭum. ākavē, furlong eṉpataip paṭaiccāl eṉṟō, aṇaippu eṉṟō, cāṉīḷam eṉṟō moḻipeyarkkalām.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamaiyāku peyarāyuḷḷaṉa. bacterium eṉṉum āṅkilac col ciṟu kuccuppōl tōṉṟum oru vakai nuṇ puḻuviṉ peyar. itu kuccu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum baktron eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉ kuṟumaip poruḷ vaṭivāṉa baktron eṉpataṉ tiripu. il eṉpatu tamiḻil oru kuṟumaip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu (diminutive suffix).
e.ṭu. toṭṭi - toṭṭil = ciṟu toṭṭi
puṭṭi - puṭṭil = ciṟu puṭṭi
kuccu eṉpataṉoṭu ip piṉṉoṭṭaic cērppiṉ, bacterium eṉpataṟku nērāṉa kuccil eṉṉum tamiḻccol ām. ciṟu vīṭṭaik kuṟikkum kuccil eṉpatu kuṟṟil eṉpataṉ koccait tiripu. cila peyarkaḷ paṉmaṭi yākupeyarkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa.
Mail eṉṉum āṅkilac col pai eṉṉum aṭippaṭaip poruḷatu. atu piṉpu, muṟaiyē. paikkuḷ iṭum kaṭitaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟaik koṇṭu cellum pukai vaṇṭiyaiyum kuṟittatu. ākavē, Mail eṉpatai añcal allatu añcalai eṉṟu kuṟikkalām.
ellāc coṟkaḷaiyum vērpporuḷ paṟṟiyē moḻipeyarkka vēṇṭiyatillai. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpu nōkki ataṟkēṟpa oru peyariṭalām. im muṟaiyilēyē, train eṉpataip pukaiviṭum ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟip pukaivaṇṭi eṉak kuṟittaṉar tamiḻp potumakkaḷ. Train eṉṉum āṅkilac col iḻu (draw) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum trab eṉṉum ilattīṉ mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatu. cycle ai mitivaṇṭi eṉpar nellai nāṭṭār. atai īruruḷi eṉṉat tēvaiyillai.
paṇṭai nāṭkaḷil ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta ellāp poruḷkaṭkum. avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟit tūya teṉ coṟ peyarkaḷai iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar paṇpaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻp potu makkaḷ. karumpu cālit tīviṉiṉṟum, pukaiyilaiyum uruḷaik kiḻaṅkum amerikka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum, vāṉkōḻi turukki nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantavai. amerikkat taṉṉāṭṭuc coṟkaḷiṉ tiripāṉa (E. tobacco), patata (E. potato) eṉṉum icuppāṉiyac (spanish) coṟkaḷait tamiḻ ēṟṟuk koḷḷavillai. im moḻiyuṇarcci piṟkālattil āriyattāṟ keṭṭatu.
cila coṟkaḷ āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum aṭipporuḷum vaḻip poruḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. pen eṉṉum āṅkilac col, tūvu (feather) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum penna eṉṉum ilattīṉ coṟṟiripu. tūval eṉṉum tamiḻc collum attakaiyatē.
āṅkilac coṟkaḷai moḻipeyarkkumpōtu vaṇṇaṉai (descriptive) muṟaiyaik kaiyāḷakkūṭātu. collector eṉpatait taṇṭalāḷar eṉṟē moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭum. māvaṭṭa āṭcit talaivar eṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, collector eṉpataiyum district administrative head allatu district chief administrator eṉṟu māṟṟal vēṇṭum. atu poruntāmai kāṇka. kāṭciyum karuttumākiya iruvakaip poruḷ kaṭkum, coṟkaḷ orumaruṅku kāraṇak kuṟiyāy iruntāṟ pōtum.
ittakaiya muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāḷiṉ, ellā āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭu kaḷaiyum tamiḻil moḻipeyarttu viṭalām. āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭu kaḷellām mika eḷiya muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkku vēṇṭuvatu tamiḻppaṟṟu oṉṟē.  (teṉmoḻi)
tamiḻ tiraviṭa vēṟupāṭu
orukālattil oru nāṭṭil orē moḻiyaip pēcik  koṇṭirunta makkaṭkūṭṭam, piṉṉarp palvēṟiṭattiṟ paravip palvēṟiṉa moḻikaḷaip pēcum palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyp  (nations) pirintu pōkalām. māntaṉ varalāṟṟu mutaṟkālattiṟ kumari nāṭṭil tamiḻoṉṟē pēcivanta makkaḷiṉam, iṉṟu intiyā muḻutum paravi, tamiḻum teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya irupatiṟkuk kuṟaiyāta iṉamoḻikaḷum pēcum makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷākap perukiyum pirintum uḷḷatu.
tamiḻ tirintatu pōṉṟē, tamiḻam eṉṉum collum tiraviṭam eṉattirintuḷḷatu. attiricol mutaṟkaṇ tamiḻiṉ maṟupeyarāka vaḻaṅkip piṉpu tamiḻaiyum ataṉ tirimoḻikaḷaiyum orē iṉamāka aṭakkuṅ kuṭumpap peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu; itu piṟanāṭṭu moḻinūlaṟiñarāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉum, iyaṟkai vēṟupāṭṭālum āriyakkalappālum nērntuḷḷa cila iṭarppāṭu kaḷāl, tamiḻait tirimoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pirittu, attiri moḻikaḷai maṭṭum tiraviṭameṉṟu kuṟikkavum, avviraṇṭaiyum potuppaṭakkuṟikkat teṉmoḻi eṉṉum collai āḷavum, vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. immuṭipiṟkut tūṇṭum tamiḻ tiraviṭavēṟupāṭukaḷum māṟupāṭukaḷum varumāṟu :

ivaṟṟāl, tamiḻ tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu mika vēṟupaṭṭateṉpatu teḷivākum.
tamiḻ tirāviṭattiṉ ciṟanta vaṭivamātal
1. tamiḻiṉ toṉmai : tamiḻaracariṉ paḻaimai.
kiṟittuvukku 1000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa pāratap pōril, utiyaṉ cēralātaṉ eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉ, iru paṭaikaṭkum cōṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatākap puṟappāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu (puṟam. 2).
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ pāratattilum, kiṟittuvukku 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vālmīki irāmāyaṇattilum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaḻaṅkuva tuḷvīḻntak kaṇṇum paḻaṅkuṭi
paṇpiṟ ṟalaippirita liṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 955)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷuraiyil, paḻaṅkuṭi - toṉṟutoṭṭu varukiṉṟa kuṭiyiṉkaṭ piṟantār ... toṉṟutoṭṭu varutal cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya reṉṟāṟpōlap paṭaippuk kālan toṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varutal eṉṟu kūṟiṉār parimēlaḻakar.
cūravātittaṉ, cipi, mucukuntaṉ, tūṅkeyileṟinta toṭit tōṭ cempiyaṉ, kāntamaṉ mutaliya cōḻamaṉṉarellām carittira kālattiṟku mika muṟpaṭṭavar. cōḻarukku muṟpaṭṭavar pāṇṭiyar. mutaṟkālattil, pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉē tamiḻulakam muḻuvataiyum āṇṭāṉeṉṟum, piṉpu oru pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ tampimārāṉa cōḻa cērar tamaiyaṉōṭu pakaimai pūṇṭu, vaṭakkē pirintu vantu cōḻacēra araciyaṅkaḷai niṟuviṉareṉ ṟum oru vaḻimuṟaic ceyti vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.



tamiḻ tirinilai
(ki.mu. 20,000 iṉṟuvarai)
kumarikkaṇṭa māntar, tamiḻiṉ aivakaic coṉṉilaik kālattilum, makkaṭperukkam, iyaṟkai viḷaivukkuṟaivu, pañcam, koḷḷai, pōr, pakai, vēṟṟiṭaviruppu, tuṇi ceyal vēṭkai, vaṇikam, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya palvēṟu karaṇiyaṅka ḷāl, kiḻakkum vaṭakiḻakkum vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkum mēṟku mākak kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp pirintupēyiṉar. vaṭakiḻakkuc ceṉṟa turēṉiyar (cittiyar) acainilaik kālattilum puṇar nilaik kālattilum. vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa āriyar pakucoṉ ṉilaik kālattilum, pirintupōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. nēr vaṭakkuc ceṉṟavar tamiḻiṉ muṉṉilaik kālattilum piṉ ṉilaik kālattilum pirintavarātaliṉ. avar ceṉṟaviṭamel lām, palavūrp peyarkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻc collāyum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāyumiruppatuṭaṉ, avar moḻikaḷum tamiḻoṭu ciṟitum peritum toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
1. ūrppeyarkaḷ
teluṅkanāṭu : ciṟṟūr (cittūr), nellūr, kuṇṭūr, orukal allatu ōrāṅkal (Warangal).
oṭṭaram (oricā) : kaṭakam (Cuttack) = vaḷainta matil, matil cūḻnta nakaram.
pampāy : vēḷūr, vēḷūrakam (Ellora), vēḷāpuram (Velapur), vēḷakam, vēḷ (kirāmam) - (Belgaum), vēḷpaṭṭi (Belhutti). vēḷā eṉpatu palavūrkaḷiṉ potup peyarāyuḷḷatu. caḷukkiyar āṇṭa pampāy maṇṭalappakuti vēḷpulam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kuccaram : tuvārakai (Dwaraka). itu erumaiyūr (maicūr) nāṭṭi luḷḷa tuvaraiyiṉ (tuvāracamuttiram) peyarāl amainta nakar. tuvar = civappu, cempu. tuvar - tuvarai = ceppukkōṭṭaiyuḷḷa nakar.
cempupuṉain tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum puricai
yuvarā vīkait tuvarai yāṇṭu  (puṟam. 201)
akattiyar tuvarāyatippōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭi paṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmaraiyum. patiṉeṇ vēḷiruḷḷiṭ ṭāraiyum aruvāḷaraiyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟu, tuvārakai, vēḷpulam, aruvānāṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṇmaiyai nōkkumpōtu naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum.
uttaramaṇṭalam : maturai (Muttra), itu talaikkaḻaka maturaiyiṉ peyar koṇṭatu. ivvaṭamaturaiyiṟ piṟanta kaṇṇaṉ (kiruṭṭiṇaṉ) eṉṉum aracaṉ iṭaiyar kulattiṟ piṟanta tamiḻa iṭaiyarpōl ēṟutaḻuvi maṇanta oru tiraviṭa maṉṉaṉē. kiruṣṇa (karuppaṉ, kariyaṉ) eṉṉum peyariṉ kruṣ eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyum, karuḷ eṉṉum teṉcol tiripē. kaḷ-karu-karuḷ. karuḷutal karuttal.
matupura eṉpatu maturā eṉat tirintateṉpatu poruntātu.
pīkār : pāṭalipuram (Patra). itu pātirippuliyūr eṉṉum tamiḻppeyariṉ vaṭamoḻip peyarppu. itu pāṭaliputtiram eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. mutalil teṉṉārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattaic cērnta tiruppā tirippuliyūr pāṭaliputtiram eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉ atu vaṭanāṭṭu nakarappeyarāy amaintatu. pātiri eṉṉum pūppeyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pāṭali eṉat tiriyum.
oṇṇiṟap pātirippūc cērtalāl  (nālaṭi. 139)
vaṅkāḷam kāḷikkōṭṭam (Culcutta). itu kāḷikōyilāṟ peṟṟa peyar.
kāḷi mutaliṟ pālainilat teyvamāyiruntu, piṉṉarp pōril veṟṟi tarupavaḷ (koṟṟavai) eṉṟum, ammai nōyai uṇṭākkupavaḷum nīkkupavaḷum eṉṟum, nampap paṭṭatiṉāl ēṉai nilaṅkaḷiṉ teyvamāyum, tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṉmutalāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaḷ. kōyilaik kōṭṭam eṉpatu paḻantamiḻ marapu, vētakkāla āriyat teyvaṅkaḷuḷ kāḷi illai. vaṅkañceṉṟa piṉṉarē āriyar kāḷi vaṇakkattai mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. kāḷi eṉṉum peyar karuppi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉcol; kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḷam-kāḷi. muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭ koḷkaiyum uruva vaṇakkamum kōyil vaḻipāṭum vēta āriyarkkillai.
tamluk. itu tamiḻakam eṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām. vaṅkañ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya paṇṭait tamiḻa vaṇikar ippeyarai iṭṭaṉar pōlum!
ūr, puram, puri mutaliya tamiḻiṭappeyarīṟukaḷ, tirintum tiriyātum iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mutalil, puram eṉpatu kōpuramuḷḷa nakaraiyum puri eṉpatu matil cūḻnta nakaraiyum kuṟittaṉa.
2. kulappeyarkaḷ
pampāy maṇṭalattiṉ teṉpakutiyil vāḻntavar vēḷir eṉṉum tamiḻa vakuppār.
vāṇiyaṉ (vāṇikaṉ) eṉṉum kuṭippeyar paṉiyā eṉṟum, ceṭṭi eṉṉum kuṭippeyar cēṭ(ṭu) eṉṟum, vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. eṭṭu - eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. eṭṭutal = uyartal. eṭṭam = uyaram. eṭṭi - uyarntōṉ. cirēṣṭa eṉpatu cirēya eṉpataṉ uccattaram (Superlative Degree).
pāṇṭava kavuravariṉ muṉṉōr katiravaṉ marapiṉarātaliṉ cōḻaṉ vaḻiyiṉarumāvar, muccuṭaraiyum mutalākak koṇṭa mūvaraca marapukaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē iruntuvantamaiyum muṉpu pāṇṭiyarum piṉpu cēra cōḻarum vaṭanāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum teṉṉāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum potu muṉṉōṉākak kūṟappaṭutalum, ataṉāl cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟamaiyum, nōkkuka.
3. centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
tamiḻar vaṭakkē cellaccella. taṭpaveppa nilaimāṟṟam, cōmpal, pulavariṉmai, tamiḻppuravalariṉmai, putiya cuṟṟuccārpu, tāynāṭṭoṭu toṭarpiṉmai mutaliya karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻ tirintu koṭuntamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. tiriyāta teṉṉilattamiḻ centamiḻ eṉappeṟṟatu. cilar koṭuntamiḻ tiruntic centamiḻāyīṟṟeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyil muṟpaṭṭa tiruntāta nilaikaḷellām kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉpākattilēyē tīrntuviṭṭaṉa.
koṭun tamiḻccoṟkaḷ potuvāy, poruḷ tirinta col, vaṭivutirinta col, olitirinta col, centamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟa col, putuccol eṉa aivakaippaṭum.
e.kā.

ammu (to sell) - vērmaṟainta col
iṉi, iṟṟaittamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟut teluṅkiṟ putuc coṟpōl tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām vērccol kumari nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṉa, eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
talaikkaḻaka aḻivum iṭaiyīṭum
tōrā. ki.mu. 4500 pōl, pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ kālattil, arapik kaṭaluḷḷa viṭattu nilappakutiyum, nāvalantīviṉ teṉpakutiyāṉa kumarik kaṇṭamum, kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa. vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭaliṉum arapikkaṭal, muntiyatākum. ataṉālēyē, kaṭal teyvamākiya vāraṇaṉai mēṟṟicait talaivaṉ eṉṟum, vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭalait toṭukaṭal eṉṟum, kūṟiṉar.
kuṇāatu karaiporu toṭukaṭaṟ kuṇakkum  (puṟam. 6).
talaikkaḻakattaip puranta pāṇṭiyar eṇpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, akkaḻakattiṉ iṟutikkālap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷurai kūṟum.
peru (mutu) nārai, peruṅ (mutu) kuruku, kaḷariyāvirai mutaliya nūlkaḷ talaikkaḻakattāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟaṉavākac collap peṟum.
4. iṭaikkaḻakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 4000-1500)
talaikkaḻakam aḻintu ēṟattāḻa 500 āṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, kumariyāṟu kaṭaloṭu kalanta iṭattil, alaivāy eṉṟō kayavāy eṉṟō, katavam eṉṟō putavam eṉṟō peyar peṟṟirunta tuṟai nakaril, veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ iṭaikkaḻakattai niṟuviṉāṉ. aṉṟum āriyarumillai; āriyakkalappuḷḷa nūlumillai.
iṭaikkaḻaka irukkaiyaik kapāṭapuram eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yilakkiyam kūṟum. maturaiyai maturāpuri eṉṟu virittalpōl, kapāṭattaiyum kapāṭapuram eṉṟu virittirukkalām. katavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu ilakkaṇappōlit tiripām; alaivāy allatu kayavāy eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu araippeyar moḻi peyarppām; putavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, atu muḻuppeyar moḻipeyarppām. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam pukār (āṟu kaṭaliṟ pukumiṭam) eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntamaiyum, keṭilam kaṭaloṭu kūṭumiṭam kūṭalūr eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷamaiyum nōkkuka.
5. koṭuntamiḻum tiraviṭamum
orukālattil koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟirunta ticaimoḻikaḷ (Regional Dialects), piṟkālattil tiraviṭam eṉṉum kiḷaimoḻikaḷāyt tirintuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻar aṟiya, mutalāvatu tirinta kiḷaimoḻi teluṅkē. atu tirinta kālam ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 1500. teluṅku nāṭṭiṟkut teṟkē nīṇṭakālamāyt tamiḻoṉṟē vaḻaṅki vantatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
āyiṭait
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālap paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyatu pōṉṟē,
neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamut toṭiyōḷ pauvamun
tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉal naṉṉāṭṭu  (cilap. 8 : 1, 2)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālamvarai vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkil tamiḻtavira vēṟemmoḻiyum vaḻaṅkātiruntamaiyaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉi, tiraviṭamum, (1) vaṭatiraviṭam, (2) naṭuttiraviṭam, (3) teṉ tiraviṭam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟai muṉtiraviṭam, iṭait tiraviṭam, piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum aḻaikkalām.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilkaḷē tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṉpu avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ pintit tōṉṟiya neṭil kaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷum pintiyē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ekara okarak kuṟilvarikaṭku mikaikkuṟi yiruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. ivviru kuṟilkaḷum tōṉṟu muṉṉarē. orucārt tamiḻar vintiyamalaiyaṭuttum ataṟkappālum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. avar moḻiyē piṉpu cūracēṉi, mākati, makārāṭṭiram mutaliru pirākirutaṅkaḷākap pirintu pōyirukkiṉṟatu. ap pirākirutaṅkaḷiṉ tiripē inti, vaṅkam, marātti, kucarātti mutaliya iṟṟaimoḻikaḷ. ivaṟṟil ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ illai. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ tamiḻāyiruppatuṭaṉ, toṭaramaippilum ivai tamiḻaiyē muṟṟilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa eṇkaḷum orumai paṉmai yeṉṉum iraṇṭē. intiyil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālē uḷa. immoḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum maraput toṭarkaḷaiyum paḻamoḻikaḷaiyum nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟaip pēcum makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr tiraviṭarāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum muṭipiṟkē varamuṭiyum.
marāttiyum kucarāttiyum orukālattil tiraviṭamoḻikaḷāyk koḷḷappeṟṟu, pañcatirāviṭattiṉ iru kūṟukaḷāyk kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. marāttiyai aṭuttut teṟkē vaḻaṅkuvatu teluṅku.
vintiyamalaikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa inti, vaṅkam mutaliya moḻi kaḷai vaṭatiraviṭam allatu muṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum, ammalai yaiyaṭutta marātti, kucarātti mutaliya moḻikaḷai naṭuttiraviṭam allatu iṭaittiraviṭam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷait teṉtiraviṭam allatu piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum, koḷḷiṉum poruntum.
iṟṟai nilaiyil inti vaṅka mutaliyavaṟṟai vaṭa nāvalam eṉṟum, marātti kucarāttiyai naṭunāvalam eṉṟum, koḷvatum poruttamām.
tamiḻ vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirintateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka, mutaṟkaṇ, teṉtiraviṭattil teṉkōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa teluṅkiṉiṉṟum vaṭakōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa pirākuvī yiṉiṉṟum, cilacoṟkaḷ īṇṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟum.

kuṟippu (1) ēṉ, ēm eṉpaṉavē taṉmaippeyariṉ mula vaṭivaṅ kaḷātalāl, yēṉ, yēm eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷē nēṉu, mēmu eṉṟu tirintirukkumeṉṟu cilar karutalām. avvāṟāyiṉ, mēmu eṉpatu nēm eṉpataṉ tiripāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
(2) teluṅkil ati eṉpatu peṇpālaiyum oṉṟaṉ pālaiyum uṇarttalāl, āme eṉpatu ā amma eṉpataṉ tokutta tākavumirukkalām.

māṟṟamāṭu eṉṉum viṉai
māṟṟam = col. āṭu eṉpatu oru tuṇaiviṉai 
māṟṟamāṭu = collāṭu, uraiyāṭu, pēcu.
māṟṟam - māṭṭa (te.). māṟṟamāṭu - māṭṭāṭu (ta.) - māttāṭu (ka.)
kaḷ eṉṉum paṉmaiyīṟu teluṅkil kalu - lu eṉṟu tiriyum.
māṭṭalu (māṟṟaṅkaḷ) + āṭu = māṭṭalāṭu - māṭlāṭu
cila teluṅkuccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭai yeḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu tokku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
aḻuttu - attu, iluppai - ippa, urukku - ukku, koḻuppu - kovvu, ceruppu - ceppu, tiruttu - tittu, curuṭṭu - cuṭṭa, neruppu - nippu, paruppu - pappu, perutta - petta, maruntu - mantu, viruntu - vintu.
uruṇṭai, oruttaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ uṇṭai, ottaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻilum tirintiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivukaḷum vaḻakkil uḷa, teluṅkil aṅṅaṉamaṉṟu.

kumppu, aṇṭṭāru eṉṟu eḻutuvatu tamiḻ marapaṉṟu.
vaṭakkē cellaccella, moḻiyolikaḷ iṅṅaṉam valuttuk koṇṭē pōkum. itaṟkut toṭakkam teluṅkum muṭivu camaṟkirutamum ākum. teluṅku vaṭa moḻipōl vallōcai peṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ, vaṭaticaiyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatum kavaṉikkattakkatu. tamiḻolikaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pōl kaṭuvolikaḷum (Surds) polivolikaḷumāy (Scnants) pōṭṭiyiruntu piṉṉark kaṭumaiyum polivum nīṅkiṉaveṉpatu, vaṉkāy mīṇṭum meṉpiñcāyiṟṟeṉṟu kūṟuvatupōṉṟatē.
ṅkka, ñcca, ṇṭṭa, ntta, mppa, eṉṉum vaṉkūṭṭolikaḷ teluṅkiṟ peruvaḻakkāy varum; tamiḻil maruntiṟkuṅ kāṇak kiṭaiyā. mutaṉ mutal vaṭaticaiyāl vaṭaku eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa tum, uṇmaiyil vaṭamoḻikku aṭippaṭaiyum, teluṅkē.
7. pirākuvīt tirivu
tamiḻ vēṅkaṭattiṟku vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirinta tuṭaṉ, tiraviṭamoḻikaḷum vaṭakkē cellaccella mēṉ mēlum tirintum, ciṟuttum citaintum, citaṟiyum, ilakkiya maṟṟum, āriyamāka māṟiyum, pōyiṉa.

kaṉā pāva hamē cuṉ urāṭṭī tūlik = eṉ appaṉār antac ciṉṉa uṟaiyil (vīṭṭil) kuṭiyirukkiṟār.
kh, gh mutaliya cila mūccoli yeḻuttukkaḷum, cār (char) tantam mutaliya inti vaṭamoḻiccoṟkaḷum, pirākuvīyiṟ kalantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
perumpāl mēlai moḻinūlaṟiñar, anākarika nilai nākarika nilaikku muntiya teṉṉum neṟimuṟaiyaik kuruṭṭut taṉamāyp piṉpaṟṟi, tiraviṭam tiruntit tamiḻāṉa teṉṟum, pirākuvī muntunilai moḻiyeṉṟum, tamiḻar vaṭamēṟkiṉiṉṟu peluccittāṉa vaḻiyāyt teṟku vantāreṉṟum, vantavaḻiyil peluccittāṉam iruppatāl aṅkattu moḻi anākarika nilaiyil uḷḷateṉṟum, teṟkuvantapiṉ tammoḻiyai vaḷarttuk koṇṭāreṉṟum, uṇmaikku muṟṟum māṟākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
oru moḻiyiṉ coṟkaḷ, muntunilai, citainilai ākiya iru nilaiyilum kuṟuvaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. pirākuvīccoṟkaḷ kuṟuvaṭivu koṇṭiruppatu, citainilaiyēyaṉṟi muntunilaiyaṉṟu. vāykaḷ eṉṉum oru colliṉ citaivai nōkkiṉum iv vuṇmai viḷaṅkiviṭum.
vaḻi - (vayi) - vāy = uṇavu pukum vaḻi. kaḷḷutal kalattal allatu kūṭutal. paṉmai oru poruḷiṉ kūṭṭamātalāl, kaḷ eṉṉum col paṉmaiyīṟāyiṟṟu.
vāy - pā (pi.) kaḷ-k (pi.). vāykaḷ - pāk (pi.) iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
8. ticaimoḻit terippu (Regional Dialectic Selection)
tamiḻil mikunta colvaḷa muṇṭu. oru celvattāy vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu, oruvakaippaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟai ovvoru putalviyum eṭuttuccelvatu pōl, tamiḻiluḷḷa oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷum ovvoṉṟai ovvoru ticaimoḻiyum kaiyāṇṭuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. ïš(bj.), maṉai(ka), vīṭu(ma.).
9. kuṭiyēṟṟap pātukāppu (Colonial Preservation)
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷaik koṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭam muḻukippōṉamaiyālum, pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷaiyum koṇṭa mutaliru kaḻakavilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭamaiyālum, iṉṟu oru cila paḻañcoṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivaic cila ticaimoḻikaḷē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
cey - kai. chey (te) = kai.
al - aṉṟu alli (ka.) = aṅku
aritu - ātu (etirmaṟaiyiṭai, nilaiyum īṟum). arutu (ma.)
nīṅkaḷ - niṅkaḷ - niṅṅaḷ (ma).
nīṉ-niṉ, nīm-nim, nīṅkaḷ-nīṅkaḷ (vēṟṟumaiyaṭi).

nūṉ (nī), nūm (nīr) eṉpaṉa nīṉ, nīm eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ mūlamā yiruntu vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa tamiḻ muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷ. nuṉ, num eṉpaṉa avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumaiyaṭikaḷ (Oblique Bases). ivaiyē marātti, inti mutaliya moḻikaḷil tu, tum eṉat tirintu, eḻuvāy vēṟṟumaippeyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyar iruvakai vaḻakkiṟkum māṟāka, neṭumutal kuṟukiya num eṉṉum tiri vēṟṟumaiyaṭiyai eḻuvāy vēṟṟumaip peyarākak kūṟiyiruttalāl (tol. 325, 326, 623). avar kālattil (ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) marātti tiraviṭa moḻiyākak karutappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉṟum. atil num eṉṉum vaṭivam muṉṉilaip paṉmai mutalvēṟṟumaip peyarāka vaḻaṅkiyirukkalāmeṉṟum, karuta iṭamuṇṭu. nakaram takaramākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e.ṭu.
nīr-tīr (pi.) nēram - tēram (koccaittamiḻ).
nēram - tēr (i.). nōkku - tēk (i.). tirumāṉ srīmat (va.).
paṭarkkaippeyarkaḷ
				orumai 		paṉmai
aṇmai: yah (ivaṉ, ivaḷ, itu) yē (ivarkaḷ, ivai)
cēymai : vah (avaṉ, avaḷ, atu) vē (avarkaḷ, avai)
vō eṉpatu cēymai īreṇṇiṟkum potu. yah eṉpatu ē eṉṟum, vah eṉpatu o eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

ivvamaitiyiṉālum, hitar utar eṉṉum cuṭṭuppeyar kaḷālum, intiyil aṇmaiccuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ icuravaṭi yiṉiṉṟum cēymaiccuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ ukaravaṭiyiṉiṉṟum, piṟantiruppataik kāṇalām. muṉmai kuṟuñcēymai yātaliṉ, ukaraccuṭṭu vaṭanāvalam eṉṉum mutuvaṭa tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu tirinta moḻikaḷil, cēymaiccuṭṭāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.

mutaṉilai	 	i.kā. muṟṟum eccamum
ā = vā 			āyā = vantāṉ, vantu.

ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuccol, tamiḻil oru kālattil muṟṟākavum iruntatupōṉṟē, ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭum iruntirukkalām.
jāttā hai, pōlttā hai mutaliya nikaḻkāla intiviṉai muṟṟukkaḷ. pōttāṭu, cepputtāṭu mutaliya teluṅku nikaḻkāla viṉai muṟṟukkaḷai oru maruṅku ottamaintiruttal kāṇka.
muṉṉilai viṉai
iya eṉṉum tamiḻ viyaṅkōḷīṟu, intiyil iyē eṉṉum matippuṟa vēvalīṟākat tirintuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. pōliyē = colluṅkaḷ, colluka.
kūṭātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ollātu eṉṉum tamiḻ etirmaṟai viṉaiccol, teluṅkil ottu - vantu eṉat tiriyum. atu piṉ intiyil mat eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. pōkavattu = pōkavēṇṭām (teluṅku) 
jāmat = pōkavēṇṭām (inti)
toḻiṟpeyarum nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccamum
tamiḻil al īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar viyaṅkōḷ eṉṉum ēval vakaiyākavum payaṉpaṭuvatupōl, intiyil ṉā īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar ēvalākavum āḷap peṟukiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. karṉā = 1. ceyal (ceykai) - toḻiṟpeyar.
2. cey, ceyyuṅkaḷ - ēval.
iṉi, karṉā allatu ataṉ tiripāṉa karṉē vāypāṭu etirkāla viṉaiyeccamākavum intiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu, ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷil ceyyal vēṇṭum allatu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatai orupuṭai ottatē.
ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉai mutaṉilaikaḷ
intiyil, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉai mutaṉ nilaikaḷ, āv (vā) jāv (pō) eṉṉum ēvalorumaiyuṭaṉ kūṭi, iṟantakāla viṉaiyeccap poruḷpaṭum. itil kar eṉṉum iṟantakāla niṟaivu viṉaiyecca vīṟu tokkatākak koḷḷap peṟum.
e.ṭu. sūṉ = kēḷ. sūṉjāv = kēṭṭuviṭṭup pō.
tēk = pār. tēk āvō = pārttuviṭṭu vārum.
tamiḻil ellā viṉai mutaṉilaikaḷum vā. pō eṉṉum ēvalorumaiyuṭaṉ kūṭi nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccap poruḷ paṭum.
e.ṭu. ceyvā = ceyyavā, pārpō = pārkkappō - iruvā = irukkavā, viḻupō = viḻappō.
māṟu eṉṉum iṭaiccol
māṟu eṉṉum iṭaiccol, ēkāra īṟēṟṟuk kaḻakac ceyyuṭkaḷil ētupporuḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu (puṟam. 4, 20, 22, 92, 271, 380, naṟ. 261).
aṉaiyai yākaṉ māṟē  (puṟam. 4)
itaṟkup paḻaiya vuraiyāciriyar attaṉmaiyai yātalāl eṉṟu poruḷuraittu, māṟeṉpatu ētup poruḷpaṭuvatōr iṭaiccol eṉṟu ilakkaṇak kuṟippum varaintuḷḷār. cēṉāvaraiyar mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaip poruṭkaṇ varum māṟu eṉṟum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar māṟeṉṉum iṭaiccol viṉaiyai aṭuttuk kāraṇapporuḷ uṇarttiniṟṟaliṉ eṉṟum, ilakkaṇa viḷakka vuraiyāciriyar mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷavāy varum māṟu eṉṟum, uraittuḷḷaṉar.
intiyil kē eṉṉum urupoṭu cērntu varum mārē eṉṉuñcol, ētupporuḷiṭaic collākavē vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. u ātmīkē mārē = anta māntaṉālē.
i patpūkēmārē = it tī nāṟṟattiṉāl.

12. vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷ āriyamāy māṟiyamai
1906 - ām āṇṭu intiya moḻiyāyvukkaṇakku (Linguistic Survey of India) eṭutta kiraiyarcaṉ turaimakaṉār, araittiraviṭa naṭaimoḻikaḷ (Semi-Dravidian Dialects) eṉṉum talaippiṉ kīḻppiṉvarumāṟu varaintiruppatu. tiraviṭarukkum tamiḻarukkum ōr eccarikkaiyāyuḷḷatu.
vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa pala tiraviṭa marapiṉar tattam iṉa moḻiyai viṭṭuviṭṭu ētēṉumōr āriya naṭai moḻiyaip pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar. halapī eṉappaṭum moḻi itaṟkoru nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭu. atu ivvāyvuk kaṇakkil marāttiyoṭu cērttuk kūṟappaṭum. atu marāttiyiṉālum cattīcakaṭiyiṉālum mikutiyum taḻuvappaṭṭa kalavaimoḻi.
iṅku, atupōṉṟa naṭaimoḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭait tiraviṭak kuṭumpattiṟkup piṟcērkkaiyākak kuṟippōm. itaṉāl attakaiya moḻikaḷil āriyattiṉ tākkuṟavu ettuṇai niṟaivākavuḷḷateṉpatai, māṇavaṉ aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. avviru naṭaimoḻikaḷum amaravōttiyaic cērnta latāṭi allatu rattāṭi eṉappaṭuvatum, naraciṅpurattilum cintu vārattilum pēcappaṭum pariyā eṉappaṭuvatum, ākum. iv vāyvukkaṇakkiṟkākat tokukkappeṟṟa kuṟippiṉpaṭi, avaṟṟaip pēcuvōr tokai varumāṟu :
latāṭi (Ladhadi) 		2,122
pariyā (Bharia)		  330
	mottam		2,452	
ivviru naṭaimoḻikaḷum muṉpu kōṇṭiyōṭu (Gondi) cērkkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. eṉiṉum, iṉṟu avai muṟṟum āriya vaṇṇamāyviṭṭaṉa.
vaṭatiraviṭattiṉ āriyak karunilai inticcoṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭaramaiti kaḷum
vaṭa tiraviṭa mūlamoḻi iṉṟiṉmaiyāl, ataṉ neṭuñ cēymaik kāṉ muḷaikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa inti iṅkuk kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟateṉa aṟika.

(2) puṇarppu viṉai (Copula)
iru (be) eṉṉum viṉai eḻuvāyaiyum payaṉilaiyaiyum iṇaikkum puṇarppu viṉaiyām.

ev-ve-ke kitṉā = ettuṇai (evvaḷavu), kahāṉ = eṅkē.

mēlaiyāriyam
vaṭa tiraviṭattaip pēciya vaṭanāvala makkaḷ. vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu mēlaiyāciyāviṟ cumēriya nākarikattaip parappiyum naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta āriya nākarikattiṟku aṭikōliyum, palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyp pirintu pōyiṉar eṉpatu, mokeñcōtarō - arappā nākarikattiṟkum cumēriya nākarikattiṟkumuḷḷa oṟṟumaiyālum, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaṭkum tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅ kaṭkumuḷḷa oppumaiyālum, mēlaiyāciya moḻikaḷilum vaṭa āppirikka moḻi kaḷilum airōppiya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
vaṭamēlai yāppirikkāvaic cērnta kāṉāmoḻiyilum, vā, pō, tūkku mutaliya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷāy amaintiruppatu, mikak kavaṉikkat takkatām.
mokeñcōtarō muttiraiyeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ mūla vaṭivaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atu avai tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭaiyāta nilaiyil vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar kaiyāṇṭa eḻuttumuṟai eṉpatai yallatu, tamiḻar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟkē vantār eṉṟu uṇarttātu. mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ  ceyyappeṟum putuppuṉaivukaḷiṉ (Inventions) paḻaiya amaippukkaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, atu intiyarē avaṟṟaik kaṇṭupiṭittār eṉṟu kāṭṭāmaiyum, kāṇka.
āriyak kuṭumpattaic cērnta airōppiya moḻikaḷuḷ, vaṭamēṟkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkivarum tiyūttāṉiya (Teutonic) moḻikaḷ, ciṟappāka āṅkila cākacaṉiyam (Anglo-Saxon), tamiḻukku mika neruṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. annerukkattaik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu :
(1)	viḻuttam perumpālum mutalacaiyil viḻutal.
(2)	taṉikkuṟilaiyaṭuttamey uyiroṭu puṇariṉ iraṭṭal.
(3)	irumaiyeṇ iṉmai.
(4)	viṉaikaḷ tuvvīṟotta īṟṟāl iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭal, e.ṭu. walked, told, burnt.
(5)	tiyyotta muṉṉilai yorumaiyīṟu paṇṭai yāṅkila vaḻakki liruntamai.
	e.ṭu. nī yirutti - Thou art.
(6)	cila aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum potuvāyiruttal.
(7)	āriyattiluḷḷa tamiḻccoṟkaḷaip potuvāka āṅkilamē tamiḻukku neruṅkiya vaṭiviṟkāṭṭal.

15. coṉmāṟṟat tolaivukkaṇippu (Glotto-telemetry)
āriyattiṟkum tamiḻukkum (tiraviṭattiṟkum) cila coṟkaḷ tām potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, avviru kuṭumpamoḻi kaṭkum vēṟu yātoru toṭarpumillai yeṉṟum, palar karutukiṉṟaṉar. pala tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ āriyattāṟ kaṭaṉmaṭṭum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi, tiraviṭakkūṟukaḷ āriya moḻiyamaippiṟkuṭ pukavillaiyeṉṟu, pērā. parō tam camaṟkiruta moḻi (The Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. itu teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻit toṭarpait talaikīḻāka vaittu āyntataṉ viḷaivākum.
oru peruntāymoḻi iyaṟkaiyākap paravumpōtu, āyiraṅkal tolaiviṟkorumuṟai ataṉ paḻañcoṟṟokutiyil ēṟattāḻa mūṉṟi loru paṅkai iḻakkiṉṟatu. atōṭu putuccoṟkaḷum putiya collamaiti yilakkaṇa vamaitikaḷum pukukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmaiyai aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittu oru moḻi taṉ mūlamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu evvaḷavu tolaivu vilakiyuḷḷa teṉṟō, ettuṇait tirintuḷḷa teṉṟō, kaṇikkaviyalum, ituvē coṉmāṟṟat tolaivuk kaṇippām.
airōppiya āriyamoḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pēraḷavu tirin tuḷḷataṟku, avai vilakic ceṉṟuḷḷa cēymai aḷavē karaṇi yamām. āyiṉum, iṉṟum avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷum piṉṉoṭṭukkaḷum perumpālum tamiḻc coṟkaḷākavē yuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. kum - kummal L. cumulus. kum - L. cum.
ik kum eṉṉum collē, com, con, col, co eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, sym, syn, syl eṉṟu kirēkkattilum, sam eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyilum, tirintu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷait tōṟṟuvittuḷḷatu. tamiḻkkūṟu āriyaccollamaippiṟ kaluntuḷḷateṉpataṟku, iḵtoṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām.
tamiḻait talaikīḻāy vaittāynta kālṭuvelārum, āriyattiṟkum tiraviṭattiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpai allatu oppumaiyaip piṉvarumāṟu kuṟittuḷḷār.
(1)	kirēkka moḻiyilum tiraviṭattilum ṉakaram uṭampaṭu meyyāka varukiṉṟatu.
(2)	īriṉa moḻikaḷilum paṭarkkaip pakāp peyarkaḷum viṉaikaḷum pālīṟēṟkiṉṟaṉa.
(3)	paṭarkkai yorumaic cuṭṭuppeyar allatu oṉṟaṉ pāṟpeyar, īriṉattilum takaravīṟu (d or t) koṇṭuḷḷatu.
(4)	ilattīṉiṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻiṟ palaviṉpā līṟu akaramākum.
(5)	īriṉattilum akaram cēymaiyaiyum ikaram aṇmaiyaiyum cuṭṭum.
(6)	pāracīkattilum tiraviṭattilum takara iṭainilai iṟanta kālaṅ kāṭṭum.
(7)	mutaṉilaimey iraṭṭittu iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭuvatu īriṉattilu muṇṭu.
(8)	īriṉattilum palaviṉaikaḷ mutaṉilai nīṇṭu toḻiṟ peyarākiṉṟaṉa.
iṟṟait tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ, piṉvarumāṟu paṉṉiraṇṭeṉac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuṟittār kālṭuvel.

tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ, potuvāka, vaṭakkē cellac cellat tirintum citaintum kuṉṟiyum ilakkiyamiṉṟiyum teṟkē varavarat tiruntiyum virintum ilakkiyaṅkoṇṭum, irukkum.
malaiyāḷam teṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyum paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ tiripumāyiṉum, tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉiṉ aṭimait taṉattāl, piṟa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiṉum mikutiyākavum aḷaviṟantum āriya vaṇṇa mākkappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam
tamiḻiṉ piṟantakam muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam eṉpatu, mukkaḻaka (muccaṅka) varalāṟṟālum, cilappatikāra aṭiyārkku nallāruraiyālum, (P.T.) cīnivācaiyaṅkār, cēcaiyaṅkār, irāmaccantira tīṭcitar mutaliyōr eḻutiya varalāṟṟu, nūlkaḷālum, eṉ mutaṟṟāy moḻi muṉṉuraiyālum, teḷḷat teḷiyat terintatu.
tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam kumarik kaṇṭamē eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa :
(1) tamiḻum ataṉoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum nāvalantēyattiṟkuḷḷēyē, vaḻaṅkutalum; teṉmoḻi vaṭakkē cellac cella āriyappāṅkil valuttum urut teriyātu tirintum oṭuṅkiyum ilakkiyamaṟṟum iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭum, teṟkē vara vara mellōcai koṇṭum tiruntiyum virintum ilakkiyamuṟṟum ceṟintum; iruttal.
(2) nāvalat tēyattiṟku veḷiyē tiraviṭa moḻiyiṉmaiyum, mēlai moḻikaḷiluḷḷa teṉ coṟkaṭkellām tamiḻilēyē vēriruttal.
(3) teṉmoḻik kuṭumpattaic cērtta tamiḻ malaiyāḷam kaṉṉaṭam teluṅku ākiya nāḷperumoḻiyuḷ muḻut tūymai yuḷḷa tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭil teṉkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkutal.
(4) tamiḻnāṭṭuḷḷum teṟkē cellac cellat tamiḻ tiruntiyum ciṟantum iruttalum nellai vaṭṭārattil colvaḷam mikutalum coṟkaḷ tūya eḷiya muṟaiyilum vaḻaṅkutal. 
	(tiruttak kalliṟkut teṟkiṭṭup piṟantavaṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkum itai uṇarttum).
	tamiḻnāṭu, tamiḻnāṭar eṉṟu pāṇṭiyanāṭum pāṇṭi yarumē peyar peṟṟaṉar.
(5)	tamiḻ, vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum teluṅku mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilumuḷḷa, vallolikaḷiṉṟi, muppāṉ perum pālum eḷiya polivolikaḷaik koṇṭiruttalum eṭṭum pattu, paṉṉiraṇṭumāka meyyolikaḷ koṇṭa moḻikaḷ āttirēliyāvilum ataṉaiyaṭutta tīvukaḷilum vaḻaṅkutal.
(6)	tamiḻ muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭaintu muttamiḻāṉapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa talaikkaḻakam 49 nāṭukaḷaik koṇṭu īrāyiraṅkal tolaivu nīṇṭirunta teṉkōṭip paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkarai maturaiyil iruntamaiyum, kumarik kaṇṭat tōṟṟattiṉ eṇṇiṟku meṭṭāt toṉmaiyum, atu varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē muḻukip pōṉamai.
(7)	teṉṉaimaram āttirēliyat tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟateṉ kiḻakkut tīvukaṭkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatākac collap paṭuvatum, kumarikkaṇṭattil ēḻteṅku nāṭiruntamaiyum, teṉ eṉṉuñ col teṉṉai marattaiyum teṟkut ticaiyaiyuṅ kuṟittal.
(8)	paṇṭait tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nīr nāyum, uraiyāciri yarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kārōtimamum (kāraṉṉam) āttirēliyāviṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa tākamēṉiyat tīvil (Tasmania) iṉṟumiruttal.
(9)	vaṇikattāl vanta iraṇṭōr ayalnāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷum nilaittiṇai (tāvara) vakaikaḷut tavira, maṟṟellāk karup poruḷkaḷum, kāla vakaikaḷum nilavakaiḷumākiya mutaṟporuḷum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappāka uriyavaiyē paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal. kuḷir nāṭṭiṟkuriya kōtumai vāṟkōtumai mutaliyaṉavum, paṉikkaṭṭi uṟaipaṉi mutaliyaṉavum teṉṉāṭṭil viḷaiyāmai; paṇṭaiyilakkiyattuc collappaṭāmai.
(10)	makkaḷiṉ nākarikat toṭakkattaiyuṇarttum aintiṇai makkaṭ pākupāṭum, kuṟiñci makaḷir taḻaiyuṭaiyum, nāṭṭāṭcikku muṟpaṭṭa ūrāṭciyum, akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum, aintiṉai nilappākupāṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅkum aṭuttaṭuttu amaittirāmai.
(11)	tamiḻ makkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟkālattiliruntu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē toṭarntu vāḻntu vantiruttalum, avarkku vantēṟik karuttiṉmai.
(12)	tamiḻar piṟa nāṭṭiliruntu vantāreṉpataṟkup paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyattil oru cāṉṟu miṉmai.
(13)	teṉṉāṭu, teṉṉar (teṉṉāṭṭār), teṉmoḻi, teṉṟamiḻ, teṉṉavaṉ (pāṇṭiyaṉ) teṉkalai eṉṉum peyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷamai.
(14)	paṇṭait tamiḻar tam muṉṉōrait teṉpulattār eṉṟaḻait tamaiyum; iṟanta muṉṉōr iṭam teṉpulam teṉṉulaku eṉṟum, kūṟṟuvaṉ teṉṟicaik kiḻavaṉ, teṉṟicai mutalvaṉ, teṉpulakkōṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruttal.
(15)	ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam colvaḷam coṟṟūymai mutaliya ellā vakaiyilum tamiḻ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉuñ ciṟantirut talum, teṉmoḻi vaḷarcciyiṉ muntu nilaikaḷaiyellām tamiḻē kāṭṭi niṟṟalum. tāymoḻi varalāṟṟaiyum iṉa varalāṟṟaiyum varaitaṟkēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷait tamiḻē tāṅki niṟṟalum.
(16) teṟku vaṭakkut teriyātavaṉ, teṟkum vaṭakkumāyt tirikiṉṟavaṉ. teṉvaṭal, teṉpalli, vaṭapalli (aṇikaḷ) mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷil, teṉṟicai muṟkuṟikkap peṟutal.
(17) eṇṭicait talaivaruḷ vaṭapāl mūvar tavira ēṉaiya talaivar kumari nāṭṭiṟkē poruttamāyiruttal.
(18)perumpālum coṟkaḷ kottuk kottākavum kulaṅ kulamākavum toṭar toṭarākavum amaintu tamiḻilēyē vaḻaṅki varutal.
(19)āppirikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ īṉ maruttuva vaḻakkam pōl, tantai paṭukkai, kuṟavariṭai vaḻaṅkiyatākac collap peṟutal. kuṟatti piḷḷaipeṟak kuṟavaṉ maruntu kuṭittāṉ.
